# 摘要

| Ref | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [^1] | [Information on trajectories: martingales and random times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20337) | 本文通过非负鞅的轨迹信息流导出精确变分恒等式，统一并量化了经典浓度不等式（如Ville、PAC-Bayes等）的松弛量，并引入“窥视惩罚”概念以处理任意随机时间下的信息代价。 |
| [^2] | [$TCP_\alpha$: Margin-Controlled Confidence estimation for reliable Music Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20326) | 本文提出了一种名为$TCP_\alpha$的置信度估计方法，通过引入边界控制惩罚，实现了正确与错误预测置信度值的完全分离，从而提高了音乐信息检索中预测的可靠性。 |
| [^3] | [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322) | 本文首次在相同部署条件下，系统比较了天花板安装的FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi三种雷达技术在卧室人体活动识别和睡眠监测中的性能，并揭示了IR-UWB在细粒度任务中的优势。 |
| [^4] | [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318) | 本文提出AI4AI-Bench，这是一个首个专门用于评估LLM代理设计训练算法能力的基准测试，通过10个冻结研究仓库和固定评估器来测试递归自我改进的可行性。 |
| [^5] | [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315) | 本文提出BERT-LER，一种统一实验室值表示与可解释性的BERT风格EHR模型，通过百分位分箱编码和集成梯度归因，在预测性能与解释性上填补了方法空白。 |
| [^6] | [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295) | 本文提出了一种分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法，在高相干字典下联合考虑字典学习和表示不确定性，以提供更可靠的物理支撑置信集。 |
| [^7] | [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285) | 本文利用PCMCI+算法从家庭睡眠测试数据中学习动态因果图，首次揭示睡眠呼吸障碍在不同性别和年龄亚组中的系统性因果结构差异，并识别出跨群体稳定的核心关系。 |
| [^8] | [Which Eviction Policy Should an LLM Cache Use? A Systematic Study Across Workloads, Capacities, and Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20280) | 本论文系统比较多种缓存驱逐策略，发现LFU在多数情况下表现最优，但所有策略改进空间有限，并通过条件打包结果解释了语义缓存中几何感知驱逐策略的优势受限。 |
| [^9] | [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258) | 提出了一种基于聚类的数据知情质心分裂方法，通过类感知先验减少分裂搜索空间，在保持分类性能的同时显著提升决策树训练效率。 |
| [^10] | [Transfer Learning in Nonparametric Regression with Deep ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20255) | 本文提出了一种基于深度ReLU网络的两阶段偏移学习迁移框架，通过共享结构与组特定偏差的加性分解，在非参数回归中实现了克服维数灾难的显式收敛速率。 |
| [^11] | [Gravitational-wave parameter estimation with machine-learning generated surrogate waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20222) | 本文提出了一种两阶段机器学习模型来快速生成引力波替代波形，显著加速参数估计过程，适用于未来第三代探测器的高计算成本场景。 |
| [^12] | [Daedalus-150M: A Convolution-Attention Hybrid Designed for CPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20210) | 该论文提出一种针对CPU推理优化的卷积-注意力混合架构，仅用6个注意力块和12个短卷积块，在少量数据上训练即超越多种更大模型，实现了高效推理和卓越性能。 |
| [^13] | [MemTrapBench: Benchmarking Cognitive Traps in LLM Memory Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20202) | 本文提出了MemTrapBench基准，揭示了即使正确检索的记忆也可能引发认知陷阱（推理固化和信念扭曲），并证明现有记忆策略在基准上均表现不佳。 |
| [^14] | [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186) | 本研究利用非侵入性脑电和对比解码器，通过默读作为代理任务，成功从约24万次单词呈现中提取出词汇和语义信息，展示了非侵入式解码内心独白的可行性。 |
| [^15] | [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183) | 本文提出了首个确定性算法，能精确计算二维奇异模型的局部RLCT，并给出了复杂度上界和广泛适用性证明。 |
| [^16] | [A Standardized Framework for Machine Learning in Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20181) | 本文提出了一个电力系统保护机器学习评估的标准化框架，定义了七个关键研究维度，并在PROTECT-90基准上进行了实例验证，旨在提高研究结果的可比性和科学性。 |
| [^17] | [Ask Self, Ask Others: Relation Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20172) | 本文提出了一种名为“关系”的标记混合原语，通过显式组织自我与交换关系来替代注意力机制，在多个参数规模下实现了更低的验证损失和更高的计算效率。 |
| [^18] | [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169) | 本文提出Task-CoEvolve方法，通过自适应选择信息量丰富的验证任务并基于部分评估估计完整性能，显著降低LLM提示工程优化中的评估成本，同时保持优化效果。 |
| [^19] | [Evaluating Neural Cartographic Relief Shading for Urban Environments: A Downtown Calgary Study Using High-Resolution DEM and DSM Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20149) | 本文通过卡尔加里市中心的高分辨率DEM和DSM数据，评估了神经晕渲系统Eduard在城市环境中的适用性，发现通过参数调整可在城市制图中产生优于传统解析法的视觉和实用效果。 |
| [^20] | [Feature Evolution and Migration during Vision Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20134) | 本文通过稀疏自编码器可视化ViT训练中的特征演化，发现特征迁移集中于早期训练且倾向于向浅层移动，深层比浅层更早稳定。 |
| [^21] | [Discrete Diffusion Inference-Time Control with Nested Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20123) | 该论文提出嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛方法（NSMC和FA-NSMC）用于离散扩散模型的推断时控制，有效解决了先前方法中的过度乐观和权重退化问题，在毒性和流畅性任务上性能更优。 |
| [^22] | [SAE-Xplainers: Rule-Based Feature Interpretation for Extreme Earth Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20117) | 本文提出了一种结合地理位置调制的稀疏自编码器和基于规则的解释器集成方法，用于提升极端地球事件深度学习模型的可解释性。 |
| [^23] | [Structured Affinity for Unsupervised Visual Class-Incremental Memory in Deep Artificial Immune Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20104) | 本文提出了一种结构化、无梯度的免疫亲和力方法，使深度人工免疫网络无需重放即可进行无监督视觉类增量记忆学习，在多个基准数据集上验证了其有效性。 |
| [^24] | [Reward-Guided Autoregressive Graph Generation for Efficient Multi-Agent Communication Topology Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20099) | 提出一种奖励引导的自回归图生成方法（RGA-Designer），通过训练奖励模型优化拓扑结构，在保持任务准确性的同时平均减少20.5%的令牌消耗。 |
| [^25] | [Orthogonal JEPA: Factorized Predictive States for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20065) | 本文提出了一种正交预测因子化框架，通过将目标状态分解为多个独立组件并分别预测，克服了标准JEPA在复杂系统中因单一状态导致的容量分配不均和梯度冲突问题。 |
| [^26] | [Let's Scale Step by Step: Compute-Efficient Hyperparameter Transfer for Large-Scale Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20061) | 本文提出了一种两步超参数迁移框架，通过宽度缩放和令牌维度外推，高效估计大型MoE模型的最优学习率，避免了昂贵的全规模扫描。 |
| [^27] | [What You Can't See Is What You Learn: Restricted Evidence Visibility Favors Compositional Generalization in Shared-Genome Language-Model Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20054) | 本研究通过限制模块间的证据可见性，显著提升了共享语言模型社会的组合泛化能力，表明信息遮挡能引导梯度训练发现更优解决方案。 |
| [^28] | [DecoVAE: a Lightweight Interpretable Trend-Seasonal VAE Framework for Efficient Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20052) | 本文提出DecoVAE，一个轻量级可解释的VAE框架，通过趋势差分正则化和频域复高斯VAE显式分解时间序列，在七个基准上持续优于现有方法，同时降低内存和计算开销。 |
| [^29] | [Auditing Cross-Lingual Fairness in Language Model Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20047) | 提出一个四部分评估框架，用于审计语言模型水印在跨语言场景中的公平性，并通过实证校准和多种测量揭示英语评估掩盖的多语言部署问题。 |
| [^30] | [End-to-end Early Classification of Time Series in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20044) | 本文提出DQeND，一种基于强化学习的端到端统一架构，在非平稳环境下联合优化时间序列分类与触发决策，显著优于传统分离式方法。 |
| [^31] | [An Inclusive and Lightweight Approach to Federated Continual Learning for Cultural Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20038) | 本文提出了一种轻量级、基于正则化的联邦持续学习策略FedCurv-DR，通过间隔更新参数重要性估计来减少通信开销，在保护文化遗产知识的同时实现高效且公平的学习。 |
| [^32] | [From Street View Imagery to Street Quality Indicators: Vision Language Inference for the Suburban 15-minute City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20026) | 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型的开源工作流SAGAI的新版本，用于从街景图像中大规模评估郊区街道质量，并以法国尼斯东北部为例进行了规划导向的实证分析。 |
| [^33] | [CLaST: Context-aware Contrastive VAE for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20025) | CLaST通过引入上下文感知的对比损失函数，增强了VAE对时间序列内部依赖的捕捉能力，从而在概率预测中显著提升了准确率。 |
| [^34] | [Systematic Evaluation of TabPFN-TS for Zero-Shot Probabilistic Heat Load Forecasting in District Heating Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20024) | 本研究首次系统评估了基于合成数据预训练的TabPFN-TS模型在区域供热网络零样本概率热负荷预测中的性能，探讨了其与真实时间序列基础模型相比的优劣及对供热动态的捕捉能力。 |
| [^35] | [Scale-Aware Pretraining of Time Series Foundation Models via Multi-Patch Token Alignment and Hybrid Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20005) | 本文提出SATS，通过尺度感知令牌对齐和混合掩码策略，在预训练时间序列基础模型中有效处理多采样频率，显著提升预测性能。 |
| [^36] | [Green BOA: Determining the environmental break-even point for ML-based data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19994) | 本文通过比较机器学习数据压缩算法在训练和推理中的碳成本与存储节省的碳收益，确定了其环境盈亏平衡点。 |
| [^37] | [Flow Matching Meets 3D Curvilinear Structure Segmentation in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19965) | 本文提出了一种基于流匹配的三维曲线结构分割模型，通过连续变换实现高效且泛化的曲线解剖结构分割，避免了扩散方法的计算开销。 |
| [^38] | [G-MARK: Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning for Cooperative Driving via Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19964) | G-MARK通过将多智能体观测转换为显式知识图谱，保留了物体来源和可见性信息，从而提升协同驾驶中的推理透明度和决策可靠性。 |
| [^39] | [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938) | 本研究提出一种基于指令的LLM分类框架，结合端点特定认证数据，有效提升安全日志异常检测的准确性，尤其针对边界情况。 |
| [^40] | [Auditing Recorded Predictive Lead Service-Line Classifications Against Physical Verification: A Statewide Study of New York](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19922) | 本研究审计了纽约州153个地区的预测性铅服务管线分类记录，发现75个地区存在零方差记录，其中7个被自身验证证据反驳，尤其是纽约市作为最大案例，其预测模型记录覆盖43,215个地址且缺乏物理验证支持。 |
| [^41] | [Multi-Source Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19914) | 本文提出MS-WDRO框架，利用Wasserstein重心融合异构图信号源并构建鲁棒模糊球，以应对源间差异和不确定性，实现稳健的图拓扑推断。 |
| [^42] | [A Layered Simplex Architecture for Large Alphabets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19908) | 本文提出一种极简的分层单纯形贝叶斯估计器，通过均匀相乘并归一化实现，无需调参，具有显式可计算的遗憾值，并在大字母表概率估计任务上与专门方法竞争，同时揭示缩放规律。 |
| [^43] | [Spike-based Belief Propagation in Nonlinear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19907) | 本文提出了一种结合脉冲神经动态与贝叶斯推断的类脑控制算法，通过非线性山地车停车基准验证，实现了实时状态更新和基于脉冲的目标导向控制。 |
| [^44] | [PETA:Parameter-Efficient Test-Time Adaptation for Virtual Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19906) | 提出了一种名为PETA的参数高效测试时自适应框架，通过构建口袋特异性负样本并利用结构数据库参考配体，无需重训练即可实现对预训练虚拟筛选模型的靶标定制，显著提升效率。 |
| [^45] | [Where Does the Union Bound Go? Best-Arm Identification and Strong FWER Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19903) | 本文澄清了最佳臂识别中联合界与多重检验的联系，指出其等价于强族错误率控制问题，并解释了多重性在不同假设定向下的表现方式。 |
| [^46] | [The impact of feature engineering and an optimisation framework for ocean colour machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19899) | 本文提出一个七层级特征工程优化框架，系统评估了数据变换对海洋颜色机器学习模型性能的影响，并在挪威沿海水域验证了其有效性。 |
| [^47] | [Reliable Neural Collapse Approximation for Open-World Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19890) | 本文提出ReNC方法，利用神经坍缩作为结构先验，通过原型过滤OOD样本并逐步细化，实现开放世界测试时自适应的可靠更新。 |
| [^48] | [Evidence Before Expansion: Reuse, Spawn, or Defer in Lifelong Expert Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19888) | 本文提出一种基于统计假设检验的决策层，使流式专家池在重用、生成或推迟三种操作之间做出有理论保证的选择，并证明了其有限时间有效性和保守性。 |
| [^49] | [Separating Covariate Shift from Mechanism Change with Two Discriminators: CJSD, a Conditional Discrepancy with an Exact Covariate-Concept Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19885) | 本文提出了一种基于条件差异和两个判别器的决策层，能严格区分协变量偏移和机制变化，并确保流式专家模型重用、创建或延迟决策的统计有效性。 |
| [^50] | [Interpretable Feature Learning for RF Fingerprinting via Polar MKANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19881) | 本文提出Polar MKAN网络，通过极性输入的分区单调编码实现RF指纹识别的可解释特征学习，显著提升解缠性能。 |
| [^51] | [EnvHarness: Awakening Static Worlds for Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19880) | 本文提出EnvHarness，一种可编程插件层，通过包装静态环境重塑其行为，并结合EnvRigger自动生成组件，实现无需修改底层逻辑或依赖昂贵验证器的动态环境适应，从而提升智能体学习效率。 |
| [^52] | [A Repeated Measurements Approach to $SoH$ Battery Modelling of Cyclic Aged Data in a Laboratory Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19879) | 本文提出一种基于重复测量和分层非线性模型的电池健康状态分析方法，通过正则化迭代广义最小二乘识别参数，有效区分测量噪声和单元间变异。 |
| [^53] | [PolicyGuide: From Guarding One Action to Guiding the Whole Workflow for Policy-Compliant LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19861) | PolicyGuide 通过将政策编译为工作流图并主动验证，在用户轮次边界提供步骤级修复，从而引导LLM智能体全流程合规，显著提升多领域任务通过率。 |
| [^54] | [Online Test-Time Adaptation for Generalizable Dynamic Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19858) | 本文提出OTTA-DGAD方法，通过记忆缓冲区提取动态原型并选择性融合源域通用模式与目标域新模式，解决了在线测试时自适应场景下可泛化动态图异常检测中域特定模式演化及数据顺序到达的挑战。 |
| [^55] | [Inadvertent Context Leakage in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19857) | 本文发现语言模型在上下文窗口中持有敏感秘密时，其普通输出会隐藏泄露这些秘密的相关性，并提出了黑盒自适应攻击方法，在八个专有模型上实现了高精度的秘密重建。 |
| [^56] | [Adaptive Probabilistic Shielding by Learning MDPs for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19836) | 本文提出了一种在MDP转移概率未知但转移图已知的情况下，通过在线学习估计转移概率并自适应计算概率屏蔽器的方法，从而扩展了概率屏蔽技术在安全强化学习中的适用性。 |
| [^57] | [FAR-DPO: Feasibility-Aware and Robust Direct Preference Optimization for Cyclic Peptide Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19808) | FAR-DPO通过结合可行性感知偏好构建与难度感知的鲁棒优化，提出了一种架构无关的框架，显著提高了环肽设计在挑战性靶标上的可行性和多目标控制能力。 |
| [^58] | [Answer-Level Trust Selection for Physical Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19807) | 本文提出一种无需微调或内部访问的模型无关框架，通过聚合八个行为诊断分数，在物理视觉语言推理中实现单个预测的可靠接受或拒绝。 |
| [^59] | [MileGPO: Milestone Inference with Local Evidence for Graph-Based Policy Optimization of Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19803) | MileGPO通过里程碑发现、可靠性校准塑形和进度对比校准三种机制，从在线回滚中提取过程级信用，有效解决了长时程智能体强化学习中的信用分配难题。 |
| [^60] | [Unsupervised Anomaly Detection Using Flow Matching on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19801) | 本研究揭示了在训练数据受污染的情况下，基于流匹配的异常检测性能高度依赖于异常评分函数的选择，其中基于轨迹的评分比单步决策评分更稳健，使得森林流方法在表格数据异常检测中表现优异。 |

| [^116] | [Recovering Nonlinear Functions of Latent Variables: A Plausible-Value Neural Network Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19282) | 本文提出PV-ANN框架，结合合理值后验抽样与神经网络，在非线性潜变量预测中有效克服测量误差导致的方差衰减，恢复了约80%的函数形状信息。 |
| [^117] | [Demons on a Budget: Adaptive Measurement Placement at the Entanglement Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19248) | 在固定测量预算下，测量布局的空间顺序（如连续扫描）显著优于随机或信息驱动策略，能大幅降低纠缠熵，尤其在相变临界点效果最强。 |
| [^118] | [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234) | 本文提出了一种新的三角模糊数距离度量d_{TR}，它通过将线性重缩放直接集成到距离计算中，实现了无需预归一化的尺度不变性，并严格证明了其满足度量性质。 |
| [^119] | [Active Spiking Perception: The Membrane Potential as a Belief State for Anytime 3D Point Cloud Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19232) | 本文提出主动尖峰感知（ASP）方法，利用膜电位作为信念状态，通过迭代决策和提前退出机制，实现高效且自适应的三维点云识别。 |
| [^120] | [TorchDCM: A Unified PyTorch-Native Package for Discrete Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19231) | TorchDCM是一个PyTorch原生的统一包，通过编译选择数据和模型规范到高效似然引擎，填补了离散选择模型在经济计量覆盖与可扩展GPU计算之间的空白。 |
| [^121] | [M3: A State-Event Generative Foundation Model for Market Microstructure Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19227) | M3是一种新的生成基础模型，通过联合建模订单事件与限价订单簿流动性的动态交互，实现了逼真的市场微观结构轨迹级模拟，并在大规模数据上展现出可预测的缩放特性。 |
| [^122] | [Causal Inference under Interference with Learned Exposure Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19224) | 本研究首次系统比较了多种传输模型在因果推断中的表现，发现即使污染预测精度相似，不同模型对暴露映射的恢复程度会导致溢出效应估计出现显著差异，强调了暴露映射学习准确性对因果推断的关键作用。 |
| [^123] | [Time-Series Retrieval for Grounding Multimodal Language Models in Remaining Useful Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19218) | 本文提出一种通过时间序列检索检索历史相似退化段，并将其与测试轨迹一起转换为视觉比较工件，从而提升多模态语言模型在剩余使用寿命预测中的性能，实验证实该方法优于非检索基线。 |
| [^124] | [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210) | 本文提出并验证了一种基于LSTM的机器学习模型，用于在传统航空电子设备上实现直升机起飞重量估算，满足航空安全标准，适用于机载关键功能。 |
| [^125] | [Asymmetric Attention Heads: Structured Head-Wise Context Allocation for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19203) | 本文提出了一种非对称注意力头（AAH）框架，通过按头或按组分配不同的上下文长度，在保持标准MHA接口的同时，实现了比全注意力更低的验证损失。 |
| [^126] | [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202) | 本文提出将上下文获取视为主动推断，通过权衡信息获取与成本来优化AI代理的提问和行动策略。 |
| [^127] | [Graphical Design of Interpretable Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18936) | 本文提出了一种基于Penrose张量符号的图形化表示方法，用于设计可解释AI架构，该符号提供全局视图并与PyTorch einsum代码一一对应，并应用于描述多种可解释模型及前沿模型Steerling-8B。 |
| [^128] | [Compress and Forget: bitsandbytes Quantization Amplifies Proactive Interference in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18578) | 研究发现，训练后量化（尤其是INT4）显著加剧了大语言模型在高干扰条件下的主动性干扰，导致检索准确性大幅下降，而INT8也有较小但不可忽视的影响。 |
| [^129] | [Continual Reasoning Gym: Diagnosing and Harnessing Shared Reasoning in Continual RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18574) | 本文提出了持续推理健身房环境，发现持续RLVR的最终性能差距主要由共享推理而非遗忘主导，并探讨了如何利用共享推理来提升持续学习效果。 |
| [^130] | [VQC-ZTI: Variational Quantum Control for Zero Trust Protection of the Tactile Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18572) | VQC-ZTI通过分离量子异常检测与策略执行，实现了触觉互联网零信任保护，在保持控制路径稳定性的同时，显著提升了风险区分精度。 |
| [^131] | [Which Negatives Matter? Ask Your Text Encoder: Adaptive Similarity Margins for Dense-Caption Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18521) | 本文提出HN-CLIP方法，通过利用文本编码器的文本-文本几何结构构建自适应相似度边界，解决了密集字幕检索中InfoNCE损失过早饱和和负样本未充分区分的问题。 |
| [^132] | [Too Sure to Be Safe: Model Calibration for Reliable Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17965) | 本文提出LoRD框架，通过从正确分类样本的潜在表示中学习可靠性模型，解决日志异常检测器中置信度校准不良的问题，确保错误预测不被过度自信。 |
| [^133] | [Agents unlock new capabilities through Switching LoRA Adapters as a Tool (SLAaaT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17034) | 本文提出一种通过让代理在任务执行中自主切换专门LoRA适配器的方法，显著提升多任务能力并减少灾难性遗忘，在合成编码任务上实现高达18倍的能力税降低。 |
| [^134] | [Automating Learner Assessment: Benchmarking Machine Learning and Deep Learning Models for EEG-Based Familiarity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16541) | 该研究通过基准测试15种模型，揭示了EEG-based熟悉度预测中时间泄漏导致的性能高估，并提出了严格的试验独立验证方法，为自动化学习评估提供了可靠基准。 |
| [^135] | [Ask to Be Sure: Informative Interactions for Confident Multi-Turn LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15949) | 本文提出了一种通过熵减少量化交互信息增益并作为奖励微调LLM的新方法，无需真实推荐即可生成策略性多轮对话，提升推荐置信度。 |
| [^136] | [When Is Shallow Enough? Adaptive Split Federated Learning with Client-Specific Sufficiency Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15639) | 本文提出了一种基于客户端充分性估计的自适应分割联邦学习框架，通过私有提示令牌跟踪客户端适应动态，动态调整分割点以优化异构客户端下的训练效率。 |
| [^137] | [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447) | 该论文发现变压器残差流中的预测方向作为特权锚点，将残差变化按与预测的接近程度进行几何和行为分层，且此现象在多种模型中普遍存在。 |
| [^138] | [LODESTAR: Trustworthy Entropy Is Navigated, Not Merely Measured -- Reinforced Polarizer Keeps a Frozen LLM from Being Confidently Misled by the Wrong Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11922) | LODESTAR通过干预文本上下文并利用第三方冻结LLM的不确定性评分，解决了最低熵规则在误导性段落上导致自信错误的问题，从而提升了检索增强问答的可靠性。 |
| [^139] | [Uncertainty-Aware Compositional Localization and Placement Assessment of Catheters and Tubes in Chest X-Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11288) | 该论文提出了一种组合式框架UCompCXR，通过局部片段检测、图聚类关联和精度加权融合，实现了对胸部X光片中导管和管道的逐设备放置评估，显著提高了检测率和校准不确定性。 |
| [^140] | [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256) | 本文通过受控实验表明，商业AI检测器无法区分合规的AI编辑与完整AI草稿，导致诚实的AI辅助使用面临更高制裁风险，而人性化工具可几乎完全规避检测，从而质疑AI检测在学术诚信中的有效性。 |
| [^141] | [Verifiably grounded machine interpretation of lunar geology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09276) | 本文展示了多模态视觉-语言模型可自动生成可验证的月球地质解释，但数值年龄定年需结合开放书检索机制，以平衡局部视觉证据与外部年代学先验。 |
| [^142] | [Graph Machine: Exploring Edge Mechanisms as an Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06834) | 本文提出图机器架构，通过显式边机制（边增强注意力和边中心引用）增强推理能力，在数独任务中优于Transformer，并发现模型能自发学习紧凑的基于边的几何构造。 |
| [^143] | [Provably Efficient Self-Calibrating Quantum Fault Tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05686) | 本文首次为自校准量子容错建立了可证明高效的理论框架，证明对于多种控制引起的错误，可通过重新利用综合征测量实现持续校准，无需中断计算。 |
| [^144] | [FinVerse: Financial Time-Series Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03259) | FinVerse是一个金融领域的时间序列预测基准，通过更现实的评估方式（如方向预测）来弥补传统误差指标在真实决策中的不足，包含大量金融时间序列数据。 |
| [^145] | [LLM Capability Limits: Static Emergence and Dynamic Boundary Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01548) | 本文形式化了LLM系统测试时涌现的部署边界，通过继承结构能力和资源索引有限实现，证明了在固定预算下能力扩展与逆转的精确条件，并引入了动态价值定价机制。 |
| [^146] | [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Explainable Early Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29530) | 本文提出一种神经符号自动化流程，通过预训练模型从语音测试中提取特征并构建贝叶斯网络，实现阿尔茨海默病的可解释早期诊断，同时发现新的语言标志物关系。 |
| [^147] | [DASH-OPD: Discrepancy-Aware Switching with Hysteresis for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29078) | 本文提出DASH-OPD，一种基于滞回和差异感知切换的在线策略蒸馏方法，能双向自适应地在学生和教师执行器间切换，以解决多轮场景中因学生错误导致的轨迹偏离问题。 |
| [^148] | [Learning Asymptotics with Convergence-Rate Guarantees using Linear Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23287) | 本文提出渐近学习理论（ALT），通过滑动线性最小二乘及其吉洪诺夫变体，为计算渐近展开中的未知参数提供了统一的优化框架，并严格证明了收敛条件与速率保证。 |
| [^149] | [Distributional Determinantal Point Process for Repulsive Clustering of Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21847) | 本文提出了一种新颖的分布性行列式点过程（dDPP），利用切片Wasserstein核在分布空间实现排斥聚类，并构建了相应的广义贝叶斯混合模型。 |
| [^150] | [Drift-Adaptive ICU Intervention Prediction: Freezing the Physiological Encoder for Auditable Model Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19020) | 该论文提出了一种双流架构，在模型更新时冻结生理编码器，仅更新治疗流，从而实现可审计的ICU干预预测，且性能与全参数更新相当。 |
| [^151] | [The Price of Hidden Curvature: Improved Lower Bounds for Bandit Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18652) | 本文通过构造具有隐藏曲率的凸函数类，首次证明了随机带状凸优化在$n\ge d^{10/3}$时遗憾下界超过线性带状问题的$d\sqrt{n}$，并在中等时间范围内匹配现有算法最优性，从而揭示了该问题的本质难度。 |
| [^152] | [Grounded verification of chemical and materials reasoning: detection is the bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17417) | 本文提出一种基于数据库的分层验证方法，发现检测是纠正化学和材料推理中错误的关键瓶颈，门控修正可将错误率从22%降至4%。 |
| [^153] | [MulTTiPop: A Multitrack Transcription Dataset for Pop Music](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08756) | 本文提出了MulTTiPop，一个包含572个流行音乐片段的多轨MIDI数据集，用于评估自动音乐转录，并发现现有最佳模型性能仍有显著提升空间。 |
| [^154] | [Generalist Vision-Language Models for Fast Radio Burst detection: a zero-shot benchmark against a specialized detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07382) | 本研究表明，小型通用视觉-语言模型在零样本条件下即可达到与专用FRB检测器相当的准确率，且通过简单修改提示即可灵活适配多类任务，无需重新训练。 |
| [^155] | [Continuous Behavioral Authentication via Multi-Expert BERT Log Analysis for Secure Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21900) | 本文提出了一种多专家BERT日志分析框架，通过解析Android系统日志并融合网络、电池和Wi-Fi行为的专家置信度，实现连续行为认证，以增强安全数据共享中的风险感知访问控制。 |
| [^156] | [Detecting Explanatory Insufficiency in Learned Representations: A Framework for Representational Vigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13172) | 本文提出了一个名为VER的概念框架，用于系统性地监测学习表示中的持久性残余结构，以区分解释不充分性与常规噪声或不确定性。 |
| [^157] | [PROBE-Web: An Interactive System for Probing Evaluation Landscapes of Knowledge Graph Completion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08926) | PROBE-Web是一个交互式系统，允许用户通过调整预测锐度和流行度偏差鲁棒性两个视角，灵活评估知识图谱补全模型，并提供传统评估、视角感知评估、可解释案例研究和评估景观探索四大功能。 |
| [^158] | [Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence: Explanatory Insufficiency as a Driver of Representation Learning and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07303) | 该论文提出“表征涌现引导理论”，认为解释不足是驱动表征层级转换的关键信号，并描述了从稳定观察到表征涌现的递归五阶段过程。 |
| [^159] | [From Prediction to Self: Developmental Conditions for Agency in Minimal Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05605) | 本文通过最小GRU系统的实验揭示了“编码缺口”现象，证明系统能隐式补偿自身行动但无法显式表征自我，并确定跨越该缺口需同时满足四个发展条件。 |
| [^160] | [GEM: Geometric Erasure by Contrastive Velocity Matching in Rectified Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00140) | GEM通过将轨迹级反学习信号转化为教师引导的流匹配设置，为修正流模型提供了一种统一吸引与排斥信号的简单高效概念擦除方法。 |
| [^161] | [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411) | 马尔可夫边界在表格预测中具有实际价值，尤其在特征空间大而稀疏时能显著提升预测性能，但通过因果发现恢复边界的过程可能带来不确定性。 |
| [^162] | [Geometric Evolution Maps: Extracting Stable Concept Probes from Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25848) | 本文提出几何演化图（GEMs）方法，通过追踪概念方向在残差流中的轨迹并确定稳定方向与交接层，解决了概念探针因方向旋转而不稳定的问题。 |
| [^163] | [Investigating the Interplay between Contextual and Parametric Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness under Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24960) | 本文提出FaithMATE统一接口，揭示参数性与情境性思维链忠实度呈正相关但不对称，其中参数性优化能跨范式提升忠实度，而情境性优化不能。 |
| [^164] | [The Concept Allocation Zone: Tracking How Concepts Form Across Transformer Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24856) | 本文提出了“概念分配区”概念，表明Transformer中概念形成是跨深度的连续过程，并通过三个指标和自动边界检测实现了对概念形成位置和过程的精确追踪。 |
| [^165] | [From Accuracy to Auditability: A Survey of Determinism in Financial AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23955) | 该综述系统性地分析了金融AI系统中从表格模型到图网络再到LLM智能体的三类非确定性来源，并通过公开数据实验量化了复现性失败，为可审计金融AI提供了系统视角。 |
| [^166] | [RequestRouter: Request-Boundary Routing for Efficient Single-GPU LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23057) | RequestRouter通过基于请求级特征动态选择最优推理模式，在单GPU上实现了显著的延迟降低和能耗节省，平均延迟加速2.10倍，能耗比降至0.48倍。 |
| [^167] | [Diffusion-based Denoising Beats Vanilla Score Matching in Parameter Estimation: A Theoretical Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22950) | 本文首次从理论上证明，对于多峰分布，基于扩散的去噪分数匹配估计器（DDSME）通过避免模式分离导致的误差恶化，优于普通分数匹配估计器，从而解释了其优越性。 |
| [^168] | [The Value of Finite Observation in Positive-Data Learning of Multiple Context-Free Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11644) | 本研究提出利用有限幺半群同态表示的有限观测作为侧信息，通过观测保护的元组可替换性，实现了从正数据中精确学习有界扇出多重上下文无关语言，并在固定分支上限下实现了多项式假设构造。 |
| [^169] | [Higher Resolution, Better Generalization: Unlocking Visual Scaling in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10546) | 该论文发现提高观察分辨率能显著增强深度强化学习的性能和泛化能力，并提出Impoola架构通过全局平均池化有效利用高分辨率视觉信息，同时避免参数爆炸。 |
| [^170] | [A Robust In-Context Model for Conservation Laws: Injecting Context into Flux Neural Operators via Recurrent Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05488) | 本文提出了一种通过循环视觉变换器将上下文注入通量神经算子的超网络架构，能够在无需显式方程的情况下鲁棒地求解各类守恒律，并保持长期预测优势。 |
| [^171] | [Transformer See, Transformer Do: Copying as an Intermediate Step in Learning Analogical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06501) | 通过在训练数据中引入复制任务作为中间步骤，Transformer模型能够学习字母串类比推理，并在多样化数据集训练下达到与前沿模型相当的性能。 |
| [^172] | [From Rebound to Remedy: Understanding and Mitigating Reward Hacking via Representation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01476) | 本研究通过环境操纵设置系统分析了LLM强化学习中的奖励黑客行为，发现三阶段反弹模式，并利用表征工程提取概念方向来追踪和缓解该现象。 |
| [^173] | [Learn for Variation: Efficient AAV Trajectory Learning through a Differentiable Wireless World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18853) | 本文提出L4V方法，通过可微无线世界模型和离散伴随递归，将AAV轨迹学习中的不连续目标转化为可导代理，实现高效且精确的梯度传播。 |
| [^174] | [Neural Prior Estimation: Learning Class Priors from Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17853) | 本文提出神经先验估计器（NPE），从网络潜在表示中学习类别频率信号，无需显式类别计数，实现了与标准对数几率调整相当的性能，并提升了少数类别的分类效果。 |

    本文通过非负鞅的轨迹信息流导出精确变分恒等式，统一并量化了经典浓度不等式（如Ville、PAC-Bayes等）的松弛量，并引入“窥视惩罚”概念以处理任意随机时间下的信息代价。

    

    arXiv:2608.20337v1 公告类型：交叉 摘要：在非负鞅的轨迹路径空间上考虑信息流，可为其导出精确的变分恒等式，即使在任意随机时间下也成立。这恢复了广泛使用的经典浓度不等式，从Ville不等式到PAC-Bayes不等式，并度量了每个不等式所丢弃的信息。受控的尾部界限本身是一种相对熵，通过链式法则分解为逐步条件散度。在三种几何结构中，丢弃的松弛量具有精确形式：Azuma-Hoeffding和PAC-Bayes界限的吉布斯倾斜，Ville和池化检验的穿越本身，以及$L^p$最大界限的支配证书。该证书的可选停止赤字逐步分解为运行最大值的Bregman散度。在路径-时间空间上，同一恒等式获得一个因子，用于定价预期：任意随机时间携带一个e过程“窥视惩罚”。配分函数可以...

    arXiv:2608.20337v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Accounting for information flow on the path space of trajectories of a nonnegative martingale yields exact variational identities for it, even at arbitrary random times. This recovers the widely used classical concentration inequalities, from Ville to PAC-Bayes, and measures what each one discards. The tail a bound controls is itself a relative entropy, resolved by the chain rule into per-step conditional divergences. The discarded slack has an exact form in each of three geometries: a Gibbs tilt for the Azuma-Hoeffding and PAC-Bayes bounds, the crossing itself for Ville's and for pooled tests, and a dominating certificate for the $L^p$ maximal bound. That certificate's optional-stopping deficit resolves per step into Bregman divergences of the running maximum. On a path-time space, the same identity gains one factor that prices anticipation: an arbitrary random time carries an e-process ``peeking penalty.'' The partition function can 
    
[^2]: $TCP_\alpha$：面向可靠音乐信息检索的边界控制置信度估计

    $TCP_\alpha$: Margin-Controlled Confidence estimation for reliable Music Information Retrieval

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20326](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20326)

    本文提出了一种名为$TCP_\alpha$的置信度估计方法，通过引入边界控制惩罚，实现了正确与错误预测置信度值的完全分离，从而提高了音乐信息检索中预测的可靠性。

    

    arXiv:2608.20326v1 公告类型：交叉 摘要：深度神经网络常常过度自信，即使对错误的预测也会赋予高置信度。因此，用户缺乏可靠的信号来判断何时可以信任一个预测。事后置信度估计通过在冻结的分类器上训练一个轻量级辅助头来解决这一问题。然而，现有的目标存在固有的模糊性：它们为正确和错误的预测分配重叠的置信度值，而决策边界附近的错误预测所获得的置信度分数与正确预测难以区分。在本工作中，我们提出了$TCP_\alpha$，一种新颖的置信度目标，通过引入对误分类样本的边界控制惩罚来解决这些局限性。我们证明了$TCP_\alpha$能够确保正确预测和错误预测的目标值之间完全分离，且分离边界独立于类别数量，并随阈值单调增加。

    arXiv:2608.20326v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Deep neural networks are often overconfident, assigning high confidence even to incorrect predictions. Consequently, users lack a reliable signal for deciding when a prediction can be trusted. Post-hoc confidence estimation addresses this by training a lightweight auxiliary head over a frozen classifier. Existing targets, however, suffer from inherent ambiguity: they assign overlapping confidence values to correct and incorrect predictions, while errors near the decision boundary receive confidence scores indistinguishable from correct predictions. In this work, we propose $TCP_\alpha$, a novel confidence target that resolves these limitations by introducing a margin-controlled penalty for misclassified samples. We prove that $TCP_\alpha$ guarantees complete separation between the target values of correct and incorrect predictions, with a separation margin that is independent of the number of classes and increases monotonically with th

    A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)

    本文首次在相同部署条件下，系统比较了天花板安装的FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi三种雷达技术在卧室人体活动识别和睡眠监测中的性能，并揭示了IR-UWB在细粒度任务中的优势。

    

    arXiv:2608.20322v1 公告类型：新 摘要：尽管无接触射频（RF）医疗监测的重要性日益增长，但不同无线技术，如调频连续波（FMCW）雷达、脉冲无线电超宽带（IR-UWB）和Wi-Fi传感，在相同部署条件下的比较研究却很少，因为现有研究通常在硬件、数据集和评估方法上存在差异。此外，天花板安装的雷达虽然在医疗环境部署中具有实际应用和成本优势，但其性能仍未得到充分探索。因此，本文基于20名参与者在六种房间布局下的同步记录，对天花板安装的FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi传感进行了受控比较与分析。所有技术均使用相同的卷积神经网络（CNN）在细粒度10类人体活动识别（HAR）任务和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务上进行评估。IR-UWB在...

    arXiv:2608.20322v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. [^4]: AI4AI-Bench：面向递归自我改进的算法设计中LLM代理的基准测试

    AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)

    本文提出AI4AI-Bench，这是一个首个专门用于评估LLM代理设计训练算法能力的基准测试，通过10个冻结研究仓库和固定评估器来测试递归自我改进的可行性。

    

    arXiv:2608.20318v1 公告类型：新 摘要：递归自我改进（RSI）探讨的是AI系统能否改进生成AI系统的过程，从而让下一代系统继承这种改进。该过程即训练算法：一个更好的目标函数或更新规则能提高每次后续运行（包括生成下一代代理的那次）的计算-能力交换率。因此，RSI是否可行取决于代理能否设计训练算法。现有基准测试并未孤立地评估这一能力：现有套件通过收集数据或调整超参数来取胜，且没有区分对运行方式的改变与对模型学习方式的改变。我们提出AI4AI-Bench，包含10个覆盖10种训练算法家族的冻结研究仓库。在每个任务中，代理有4小时在一台B300上重写训练算法；其代码随后从头重新运行最多12小时，并由一个对代理隐藏的固定评估器评分。

    arXiv:2608.20318v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, 
    
[^5]: 面向结构化电子健康记录临床预测任务的可解释Transformer模型

    Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)

    本文提出BERT-LER，一种统一实验室值表示与可解释性的BERT风格EHR模型，通过百分位分箱编码和集成梯度归因，在预测性能与解释性上填补了方法空白。

    

    基于结构化电子健康记录（EHRs）的预测模型仍然是医疗机器学习领域的核心，但很少有模型同时强调定量实验室信息和针对输入医疗事件的可解释性。我们提出了BERT-LER，一种用于编码EHR时间线的BERT风格模型，该模型在包含7500万患者的去标识化EHR数据集上进行预训练和微调，将实验室检测结果编码为离散标记，同时通过基于百分位的分箱保留分级信息，并结合集成梯度方法，提供基于输入EHR序列的标记级归因。我们在公开的EHRShot基准套件以及基于真实数据的哮喘严重程度进展研究上评估了我们的方法。这通过将实验室值表示和可解释性统一在单一框架中，解决了EHR基础风格建模中的方法论空白，同时评估了预测性能是否得到提升。

    arXiv:2608.20315v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive perform
    
[^6]: 高相干字典下的物理支撑置信集

    Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)

    本文提出了一种分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法，在高相干字典下联合考虑字典学习和表示不确定性，以提供更可靠的物理支撑置信集。

We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search.     [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)

    本文提出了首个确定性算法，能精确计算二维奇异模型的局部RLCT，并给出了复杂度上界和广泛适用性证明。

    

    经典信息准则如贝叶斯信息准则（BIC）依赖于正则性假设，这些假设在奇异模型中失效，导致在深度学习等场景下的模型选择不正确。广泛适用的贝叶斯信息准则（WBIC）依赖于局部学习系数$\lambda$，在解析情况下，该系数与模型的Kullback-Leibler散度的局部实对数规范阈值（RLCT）一致，以捕捉正确的边际似然渐近性。学习系数的精确计算仅限于特殊情况，通常只能使用基于采样的估计方法。我们提出了第一个确定性算法，能够精确计算任何二维模型的局部RLCT，其中模型的Kullback-Leibler距离与多项式接触等价，我们推导出其复杂性上界，并展示了其在广泛模型类别中的有效性，附有应用示例。

    arXiv:2608.20183v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $\lambda$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. Protection task, physical scope, measurements, timing, targets, preprocessing, and validation often vary jointly and remain incompletely specified. This paper proposes a standardization-oriented framework that treats evaluation design as part of the scientific contribution. Prior work in this domain has focused on particle-based methods such as best-of-$n$ sampling and bootstrap sequential Monte Carlo, which may suffer from overoptimism and weight degeneracy, respectively. We address these limitations using \emph{nested} sequential Monte Carlo methods. We formulate nested SMC (NSMC) and fully-adapted nested SMC (FA-NSMC) for Feynman--Kac steering, identifying and correcting errors in prior formulations that lead to biased final estimates. However, its training objective provides no explicit incentive for the model to generate sparse and efficient topologies. We address this limitation by introducing a Reward-Guided Autoregressive Graph Generation (RGA-Designer) inspired by Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). We train a reward model that jointly captures task correctness and structural compactness, and then fine-tune the pretrained graph generator using the reward model as feedback. Our method preserves task accuracy at the level of ARG-Designer while reducing token consumption by an average of 20.5%.

    概率时间序列预测仍然具有挑战性，主要因为建模不同的趋势和季节动态需要专门的方法。现有方法往往无法捕捉这些组件的独特内在属性，缺乏可解释性，或遭受沉重的内存和运行时间开销。为解决这些限制，我们提出了DecoVAE，一种轻量级可解释趋势-季节VAE框架，通过应用领域特定的归纳偏置，将时间序列显式分解为趋势和季节组件。趋势流通过潜在轨迹上的差分正则化器强制执行结构平滑性，类似于Hodrick-Prescott滤波器。同时，季节流通过复高斯VAE在频域中操作，天然捕捉周期性模式的幅度和相位。在七个真实世界基准上的广泛评估表明，DecoVAE始终优于现有方法。

To address these limitations, we propose DecoVAE, a lightweight interpretable trend-seasonal VAE framework that explicitly decomposes time series into trend and seasonal components by applying domain-specific inductive biases. The trend stream enforces structural smoothness using a differential regularizer on the latent trajectory, analogous to the Hodrick-Prescott filter. Concurrently, the seasonal stream operates in the frequency domain via a complex Gaussian VAE, natively capturing the amplitude and phase of periodic patterns.     摘要：arXiv:2608.20044v1 公告类型：新 摘要：时间序列早期分类（ECTS）要求在本质上在线且不断演变的环境中尽早做出准确决策。然而，大多数现有方法假设平稳性，并依赖于分离式设计，其中分类和触发决策被独立优化，这一假设从根本上限制了它们在漂移情况下的适应性。在这项工作中，我们挑战了这一范式，并研究了非平稳条件下的ECTS。我们首次在受控漂移场景中对分离式和端到端方法进行了系统比较。基于强化学习，我们引入了DQeND，这是一种统一架构，可联合学习表示、分类和触发决策，同时保持与最先进的分离式基线直接可比。在各种漂移情况下，DQeND在多种非平稳场景中展现出强大的鲁棒性，始终优于分离式方法。

    arXiv:2608.20044v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Early Classification of Time Series (ECTS) requires making accurate decisions as early as possible in inherently online and evolving environments. Yet, most existing methods assume stationarity and rely on separable designs, where classification and triggering are optimized independently, an assumption that fundamentally limits their adaptability under drift. In this work, we challenge this paradigm and study ECTS under non-stationary conditions. We provide the first systematic comparison between separable and end-to-end approaches across controlled drifting scenarios. Building on Reinforcement Learning, we introduce DQeND, a unified architecture that jointly learns representation, classification, and triggering decisions, while remaining directly comparable to state-of-the-art separable baselines. The method accumulates parameter-importance estimates across clients and experiences to protect learned knowledge, while updating them only at fixed intervals to minimize communication and computation overhead. Depth is the only structural parameter, and averaging over depths eliminates the need to tune it. Second, the regret of the resulting mixture, the excess code length it pays relative to a code that knows the source, admits an explicit and efficiently computable expression. By combining a biologically inspired spiking neural model with Bayesian inference principles, we propose a brain-like control algorithm capable of operating in uncertain environments. We use the mountain car parking problem as a benchmark with non-linear dynamics. Our results demonstrate that the proposed controller can successfully update states in real time and generate goal-directed action plans through spike-driven dynamics. The results highlight the proposed model's potential as a bridge between computational neuroscience and probabilistic control theory.

[^45]: 联合界去哪里了？最佳臂识别与强族错误率控制

    Where Does the Union Bound Go? Best-Arm Identification and Strong FWER Control

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19903](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19903)

    本文澄清了最佳臂识别中联合界与多重检验的联系，指出其等价于强族错误率控制问题，并解释了多重性在不同假设定向下的表现方式。

    

    在固定置信度最佳臂识别中，证明通常使用跨竞争臂的联合界。从多重检验的角度看，这可能令人困惑：如果最佳臂是唯一的，那么形式为“臂$i$是最佳”的假设中只有一个可以为真。为什么还需要$K-1$的Bonferroni型因子呢？答案在于有两种自然的假设定向方式。在一种定向下，最佳臂识别本质上是一个具有$K-1$个真零假设的强族错误率（FWER）问题。在相反的定向下，恰好有一个零假设为真，但成对实现可能通过$K-1$个比较中的任何一个错误地拒绝该唯一真零假设。因此，多重性并未消失，只是出现在不同的地方。本说明在两个领域的术语中明确阐述了这种等价性。

    arXiv:2608.19903v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: In fixed-confidence best-arm identification, proofs often use a union bound across the competing arms. From a multiple-testing point of view this can look puzzling: if the best arm is unique, only one hypothesis of the form ``arm $i$ is best'' can be true. Why then should there be a Bonferroni-type factor of $K-1$? The answer is that there are two natural ways to orient the hypotheses. In one orientation, best-arm identification is literally a strong familywise-error-rate (FWER) problem with $K-1$ true nulls. In the opposite orientation, exactly one null is true, but a pairwise implementation can falsely reject that one null through any of $K-1$ comparisons. Thus the multiplicity has not disappeared; it just pops up in different places. This note makes the equivalence explicit in the terminology of both communities.

    本文提出ReNC方法，利用神经坍缩作为结构先验，通过原型过滤OOD样本并逐步细化，实现开放世界测试时自适应的可靠更新。

    

    测试时自适应（TTA）方法旨在弥合源域和目标域之间的域差距。然而，当标签分布偏移发生时，传统的TTA方法变得无效，这一挑战通常被称为开放世界场景。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为可靠神经坍缩近似（ReNC）的新方法，用于开放世界测试时自适应（OWTTA）。具体来说，我们利用神经坍缩作为可靠目标域自适应的结构先验。在此先验的指导下，我们证明了预训练分类器权重可以作为源域的原型。通过测量样本与原型之间的相似性，我们过滤掉分布外（OOD）样本，以实现可靠的更新。此外，我们提出了一种神经坍缩近似机制来细化这些原型，确保它们能够在保持神经坍缩结构的同时逐步适应目标域。

In this paper, we introduce a new method named Reliable Neural Collapse approximation (ReNC) for Open-World Test-Time Adaptation (OWTTA). Specifically, we leverage neural collapse as a structural prior for reliable target-domain adaptation. Guided by this prior, we justify that the pre-trained classifier weights can serve as the prototypes of the source domain. By measuring the similarity between samples and prototypes, we filter out the Out-Of-Distribution~(OOD) samples for reliable updates. Furthermore, we propose a neural collapse approximation mechanism to refine these prototypes, ensuring they can gradually adapt to the target domain while maintaining the neural collapse structure

    本文提出一种基于统计假设检验的决策层，使流式专家池在重用、生成或推迟三种操作之间做出有理论保证的选择，并证明了其有限时间有效性和保守性。

    

    摘要：arXiv:2608.19888v1 公告类型：交叉 摘要：维护专家模型池的流式系统必须反复决定是否对到达的数据重用现有专家、生成新专家或推迟决策。我们提出了一个决策层，使这三种结果都具有统计意义。重用和生成被表述为基于条件（机制级）差异的单侧序列假设，并通过一个无差别区间分隔；推迟则是在两个投注e-process均未积累足够证据时的状态。我们证明了可预测判别器序列的可观测替代差异的有限时间随时有效性，以及到总体量的无条件单侧转移，其中每侧的松弛度是单个判别器的超额风险；经验观察到的向下偏差规律使生成侧恰好保守。通过重启的e检测器获得不牺牲保证的时效性：一组无窗口投注。

    arXiv:2608.19888v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Streaming systems that maintain a pool of expert models must repeatedly decide whether to reuse an existing expert for arriving data, spawn a new one, or defer. We present a decision layer that makes all three outcomes statistically meaningful. Reuse and spawn are posed as one-sided sequential hypotheses on a conditional (mechanism-level) discrepancy, separated by an indifference zone; defer is exactly the state in which neither betting e-process has accumulated sufficient evidence. We prove finite-time anytime validity for the observable surrogate discrepancy of a predictable discriminator sequence, and an unconditional one-sided transfer to the population quantity in which each side's slack is the excess risk of a single discriminator; an empirically observed downward-bias regularity makes the spawn side exactly conservative. Recency without sacrificing the guarantee is obtained by a restarted e-detector: a bank of unwindowed betting

    射频（RF）指纹识别通过硬件引起的I/Q损伤来认证无线设备，通常使用深度学习特征提取器，这些提取器准确但不透明，限制了它们在安全关键环境中的使用。我们提出了极性单调Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（Polar MKAN），这是一种在极性输入上构建的块分区单调编码器，其中每个潜在维度仅依赖于幅度或相位，从而通过构造实现通道分离和单调响应。在合成增益和载波频率偏移（CFO）基准测试中，Polar MKAN达到了57.2%的DCI解缠分数，而未分区基线最多仅为12.9%。我们进一步评估了真实数据上的检测准确率权衡以及对盲CFO补偿的敏感性。

    arXiv:2608.19881v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Radio frequency (RF) fingerprinting authenticates wireless devices from hardware-induced I/Q impairments, typically with deep learning feature extractors that are accurate but opaque, limiting their use in security critical settings. We propose Polar Monotonic Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (Polar MKAN), a block partitioned monotonic encoder on polar inputs in which each latent dimension depends exclusively on magnitude or phase, yielding channel separation and monotone responses by construction. On a synthetic gain and carrier frequency offset (CFO) benchmark, Polar MKAN reaches 57.2 percent DCI Disentanglement versus at most 12.9 percent for unpartitioned baselines. We further evaluate the detection accuracy trade off on real data and the sensitivity to blind CFO compensation.

    本文提出EnvHarness，一种可编程插件层，通过包装静态环境重塑其行为，并结合EnvRigger自动生成组件，实现无需修改底层逻辑或依赖昂贵验证器的动态环境适应，从而提升智能体学习效率。

    本文发现语言模型在上下文窗口中持有敏感秘密时，其普通输出会隐藏泄露这些秘密的相关性，并提出了黑盒自适应攻击方法，在八个专有模型上实现了高精度的秘密重建。

    

    arXiv:2608.19857v1 公告类型：新 摘要：为了让AI代理在简单聊天之外更有用，它们必须持有敏感的用户上下文，如日历、凭证、健康记录和财务数据。我们研究的是，这类秘密仅仅出现在模型的上下文窗口中，是否会在模型的无害输出中引入隐藏相关性，使得即使模型正确拒绝直接提取，也能重建这些秘密。我们进一步研究，对手是否能主动设计提示词来放大这种效应，利用模型作为隐蔽载体，通过看似无害的文本传输秘密。在这两种情况下，这种有限的泄露都通过一种新颖的自适应攻击被利用，该攻击假设对底层模型具有黑盒访问权限。在对八个专有模型进行的受控实验中，我们发现，2位数的上下文秘密能以近乎完美的准确率被重建，4位数秘密的精确匹配率达到82%，所有这些都来自模型对普通、非对抗性提示产生的输出。

We further study whether an adversary can actively engineer prompts that amplify this effect, using the model as a covert carrier to transmit secrets through seemingly innocuous text. In both cases, this limited leakage is exploited using a novel adaptive attack that assumes black-box access to the underlying model.     [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19836](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19836)

    本文提出了一种在MDP转移概率未知但转移图已知的情况下，通过在线学习估计转移概率并自适应计算概率屏蔽器的方法，从而扩展了概率屏蔽技术在安全强化学习中的适用性。

    

    arXiv:2608.19836v1 公告类型：交叉 摘要：概率屏蔽是一种用于安全强化学习（RL）的技术。通常，一个静态观察器——称为屏蔽器——将学习代理的动作限制为那些保持安全可行的动作。传统上，屏蔽器是根据底层马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的转移概率计算的。因此，当MDP模型未预先给定时，该技术不适用，而这在典型的RL应用中恰恰是常见情况。在本文中，我们研究了在MDP转移图已知但转移概率未知的情况下计算屏蔽器的问题。我们的方法将概率屏蔽与在线模型学习相结合：当RL代理探索环境时，我们估计转移概率。根据此估计，我们计算一个屏蔽器。虽然屏蔽器最初可能较为保守，但随着模型估计变得更加精确，它会自适应调整。

    arXiv:2608.19836v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Probabilistic shielding is a technique for safe reinforcement learning (RL). Typically, a static observer -- called the shield -- constrains the learning agent's actions to those for which acting safely remains feasible. Traditionally, the shield is computed from the transition probabilities of the underlying Markov decision process (MDP). Thus, this technique is not applicable when the MDP model is not given a priori, which, unfortunately, is the case in typical RL applications. In this paper, we study the problem of computing a shield in the setting where the transition graph of the MDP is known, but the transition probabilities are unknown. Our approach integrates probabilistic shielding with online model learning: as the RL agent explores the environment, we estimate the transition probabilities. To address these limitations, we propose FAR-DPO (Feasibility-Aware and Robust Direct Preference Optimization), an architecture-agnostic framework that steers generative models toward structurally and biophysically feasible cyclic peptide designs, particularly for challenging targets.     世界模型提供了真实世界的数字模拟，使代理能够在昂贵的现实部署之前进行训练和测试。在每个时间步，它们接收一个动作并生成与真实世界统计匹配的观测和奖励。在复杂环境中，当前结果取决于遥远的过去事件，这需要记忆。人们可能期望，通过增加记忆，我们总能构建一个足够准确的模型，以使真实和虚拟世界的最优代理策略对齐。我们表明，对于经典世界模型，即使真实世界本身是经典的，这也是错误的。我们构造了真实世界，其中每个有限经典模型沿着相同的可能轨迹失败：它要么在真实世界明显偏好某个动作时失去区分动作的能力，要么反复将最高期望奖励分配给次优动作。其期望奖励估计也保留了一个不可消失的...

Linearizing the joint dynamics yields a signed response operator that maps a localized gradient perturbation to its future loss effects, revealing how optimizer memory shapes their magnitude, timing, and sign. We further derive an exact multistep error decomposition and establish first-order finite-horizon accuracy under local smoothness and controlled activation switching. Experiments validate the response mechanism and optimizer-state effects, while repeated-future analyses reveal substantial

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19748)

    本文提出TUP策略，通过截断低排名完成并仅重加权高排名部分，以更稳健且高效的方式蒸馏Best-of-N推理选择方法，支持离线训练和闭式归一化。

    

    推理时选择方法，如最佳N项（Best-of-N），通过采样一组候选并根据奖励模型选择排名最高的完成来改进生成。蒸馏旨在通过将原始奖励替换为池内排名，并学习一个提升高排名完成的策略，将此过程摊销为单一策略。然而，现有的基于排名的策略通常使用平滑的全支持重加权，因此低排名完成获得较少权重但仍保留在目标支持中。尽管更尖锐的重加权减少了低尾权重，但也增加了对单一奖励模型在顶部做出的脆弱排名的依赖。我们提出TUP：一种截断劣质、提升优质策略，它从支持中移除低排名完成，并仅对保留的上尾部分进行可调尖锐度的重加权。TUP支持闭式、与提示无关的归一化，并可通过二元交叉熵完全离线训练。

    arXiv:2608.19748v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Inference-time selection methods, such as Best-of-N, improve generation by sampling a pool of candidates and selecting the top-ranked completion according to a reward model. Distillation seeks to amortize this procedure into a single policy by replacing raw rewards with in-pool ranks and learning a policy that upweights higher-ranked completions. However, existing rank-based policies typically use smooth full-support reweighting, so low-ranked completions receive less mass but remain in the target support. Although a sharper reweighting reduces lower-tail mass, it also increases reliance on brittle ranking at the top made by a single reward model. We propose TUP: a Truncate-bad, Upweight-good Policy that removes low-ranked completions from the support and reweights only the retained upper tail with a tunable sharpness. [^68]: 问题引导的多模态视觉问答证据获取

    arXiv:2608.19739v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Multimodal LLMs can see a document, but they often can't read it reliably. Small text, tables, visual cues, and topological elements still trip them up under direct visual inference, even when the page is already sitting in the model's context. Most document-VQA systems treat perception as fixed: they encode the page once, ask the question, and answer from whatever the model happened to extract in that single fast pass. We think document VQA needs slower, more deliberate perception: rather than answering from one fixed encoding, the model should spend a bit of extra compute at inference time working out what to look at next, and only then answer. We build this into \textbf{Q-Guide}, a small agent that reads a question, works out what evidence it is still missing, and calls targeted tool(s) to recover it---reading text where text is needed, zooming in where detail is needed, or grounding a region where position matters. Guided by this principle, we propose L-VQVAE, a generative model in which each discrete token is produced from a short contiguous window, and LVQMark, a watermarking method over this token space that combines logit-bias injection with robust re-encoding for attack-time detection.     我们开发了一个几何框架，用于通过神经网络学习确定性和随机受迫哈密顿系统。受拉格朗日-达朗贝尔原理和变分积分器理论的启发，我们引入了拉格朗日-达朗贝尔映射的概念，并为一阶单步方法建立了C^r收敛定理。基于这些结果，我们提出了广义受迫哈密顿神经网络（GFHNNs），这是一类通过串联拉格朗日-达朗贝尔-欧拉映射获得的结构保持神经网络，并证明了该架构的通用逼近定理。我们进一步将框架扩展到参数依赖系统，从而引出参数化广义受迫哈密顿神经网络（PGFHNNs）。通过将Stratonovich-泰勒展开中出现的多个Stratonovich积分解释为参数，同一框架也可应用于随机受迫哈密顿系统。

    该论文发现，在物理信息神经网络中，技术改进并非简单累加，只有特定组合（SIREN激活与因果加权）能显著提升性能，而叠加其他技术会导致性能急剧恶化，揭示了干预措施间的非线性交互效应。

    本文提出了一种高效的重采样分析方法，揭示了文本生成中不确定性动态的稳定模式，并表明噪声主要源于采样而非模型对单个标记的敏感性。

    

    大型语言模型的推理具有随机性，因此理解一个模型需要处理其对给定问题可能产生的推理链分布，即其不确定性。基于重采样的分析刻画了这一分布，揭示了在生成过程中哪些步骤决定了模型如何得出答案。然而，这些方法的一个主要局限是，在推理链中每个标记或句子处重采样文本序列的成本非常高。我们的工作旨在使重采样分析在计算上更高效，同时揭示一个重要的科学问题：解释文本生成中不确定性动态的正确统计模型是什么？我们表明，当重采样多个推理链时，不确定性动态会收敛到稳定模式，而噪声主要来自采样的伪影，而非模型对每个单独标记或推理步骤的敏感性。我们开发了一种统计方法来实现这一目标。

However, a major limitation of these approaches is that resampling text sequences at every token or sentence in a reasoning chain is very costly. Our work strives to make resampling analysis more computationally efficient, while also shedding light on an important scientific question: what is the right statistical model for explaining uncertainty dynamics in text generation? We show that when resampling many reasoning chains, uncertainty dynamics converge to stable patterns, and noise is largely an artifact of sampling rather than an LLM's sensitivity to each individual token or reasoning step. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears.     arXiv:2608.19463v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations.     摘要：通过网络传播的冲击，如网络安全漏洞披露，可能突然中断金融时间序列并导致重大异常损失。虽然这些事件通过新闻报道、监管文件或公共数据库以离散记录的形式披露，但其后果通过持续的市场动态展开。这产生了一个事件条件影响预测问题：给定事件前的市场历史和有限的事件元数据，目标是估计披露后的短期异常损失，而非重建完整的事件后轨迹。然而，大多数时间序列预测模型侧重于内生规律，如趋势、季节性和自相关性，因此难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件。这一挑战因稀疏的高影响事件和背景市场噪声而进一步加剧。我们引入了EventTime，一个多分辨率框架，结合了长期市场协同...

    arXiv:2608.19447v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. We show that learned policies exhibit rich state-dependent behaviour, allocating liquidity according to mispricing, rebalancing costs, uncertainty, inventory exposure, and heterogeneous risk preferences. In this framework, we compute the tree-level neuron-neuron propagator which describes the evolution of layer variance in the network, and develop the Feynman diagram machinery to compute interactions in the perturbative expansion in $1/N$.     不确定性量化（UQ）对于大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全部署至关重要。现有方法，从言语化置信度到需要多次生成的方法，通常是零样本的，并产生量化不确定性的分数，无需标记数据。然而，在实践中，部署前必须在感兴趣的数据集上评估其性能。在这项工作中，我们表明，通过利用该数据集，我们始终优于这些现有分数。具体来说，我们构建了简单的分类器，通过使用这些分数和相似查询的正确性作为特征来预测LLM响应的正确性。我们的方法产生最小的计算开销，使其成为现实世界应用中LLM UQ的一种廉价且直接的增强手段。

    arXiv:2608.19323v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

    arXiv:2608.19234v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge.     本文提出将上下文获取视为主动推断，通过权衡信息获取与成本来优化AI代理的提问和行动策略。

    

    交互式AI代理必须尽可能高效地获取正确的上下文。当用户遗漏约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，代理可以继续使用默认假设，或花费令牌进行澄清提问、检索调用、工具调用或提示试验。我们将这种权衡表述为上下文获取的主动推断。内部推断步骤更新对潜在任务状态的信念，外部决策选择下一个上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作，以在成本下最小化预期自由能。在确定性设置中，认知项简化为预期信息增益，可选择按令牌成本进行归一化。我们在最优提问（OQA）中实例化该框架，使用精确后验和动态规划预言机，并在25至300个候选项的二元和多类别分类任务上对前沿语言模型进行基准测试。我们还研究了生成前的澄清和自动化设置。

    arXiv:2608.19202v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automa

    设计、实现和比较可解释架构需要一个形式化语言来表示它们。最常见的表示方式在两方面存在不足。符号方程无法一目了然地提供架构的全局视图。概率图模型和流程图不描述实际的张量操作，从而隐藏了关键见解并限制了可复现性。为弥合这一差距，我们引入了一种用于设计可解释AI架构的图形化符号，该符号改编自Penrose张量符号。这种图形化符号提供了架构的全局视图，并与PyTorch einsum代码一一对应。我们首先使用此符号来描述按构造可解释的架构，包括概念瓶颈、稀疏探针、原型网络、神经加性模型和线性模型混合。然后，我们图解了前沿可解释语言模型Steerling-8B的关键架构组件。

Probabilistic graphical models and flowcharts do not describe actual tensor manipulations, thus hiding key insights and limiting reproducibility. To close this gap, we introduce a graphical notation for designing interpretable AI architectures, adapted from Penrose tensor notation. This graphical notation gives a global view of an architecture and maps one to one onto PyTorch einsum code. We first use this notation to describe architectures that are interpretable by construction, including concept bottlenecks, sparse probes, prototype networks, neural additive models, and mixtures of linear models.     研究发现，训练后量化（尤其是INT4）显著加剧了大语言模型在高干扰条件下的主动性干扰，导致检索准确性大幅下降，而INT8也有较小但不可忽视的影响。

    

    arXiv:2608.18578v1 公告类型：新公告 摘要：主动性干扰（PI）是大语言模型中一个已被记录的性能缺陷，即当重复覆盖的值被检索时，其准确性会随着先前覆盖的累积而下降，这反映了人类工作记忆中的经典现象。训练后量化（PTQ）现在是开放权重模型默认的部署路径，但其对这种缺陷的影响尚未被测试。我们通过bitsandbytes评估了三种精度水平（FP16、INT8、INT4/NF4），涵盖三个架构不同的指令调优模型（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3、Phi-3.5-mini-instruct），并保持检索任务固定。INT4量化在高干扰条件下显著降低了每个模型的准确性（例如，Qwen从81.0%降至68.3%），通过配对McNemar检验（$p \le 2.6 \times 10^{-6}$）和覆盖所有干扰水平的混合效应回归确认；INT8虽然通常被认为是安全的，但也带来了较小但真实的性能损失。

Post-training quantization (PTQ) is now the default deployment path for open-weight models, yet its effect on this failure mode has not been tested. We evaluate three precision levels (FP16, INT8, INT4/NF4, via bitsandbytes) across three architecturally distinct instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini-instruct), holding the retrieval task fixed. INT4 quantization significantly reduces accuracy under high interference in every model (e.g., from 81.0% to 68.3% for Qwen), confirmed by paired McNemar's tests ($p \le 2.6 \times 10^{-6}$) and a mixed-effects regression spanning all interference levels; INT8, often assumed safe, also carries a smaller but real penalty i
    
[^129]: 持续推理健身房：诊断与利用持续RLVR中的共享推理

    本文提出了持续推理健身房环境，发现持续RLVR的最终性能差距主要由共享推理而非遗忘主导，并探讨了如何利用共享推理来提升持续学习效果。

To answer this question, we introduce Continual Reasoning Gym, a continual-RLVR environment that organizes text and visual reasoning tasks into five task sequences. In this setting, we identify two key observations: Sequential RLVR exhibits modest forgetting, yet its final performance remains below that of MTRL. To understand the latter, we decompose final performance and show that forgetting accounts for only part of the gap.     arXiv:2608.17034v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Post-training can unlock new capabilities and improve performance on specialized tasks, but sometimes at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in other domains. This poses a problem in long agent trajectories that compose different capabilities. We reject this tradeoff by giving an agent a tool to switch between specialized LoRA adapters mid-trace. To test its effectiveness, we compose two synthetic coding tasks that are logically simple but require specialization. We find that this allows the model to solve problems it previously could not, that the model is able to switch autonomously (and find a new strategy that beats our human heuristic baseline on one task), and that this incurs an up to an 18x reduction in capability tax compared to an agent using only one specialized adapter. Our approach also substantially outperforms spawning subagents in both task capabilities and token usage.

The model places a dDPP prior over the atoms of the mixing measure and defines a generalized likelihood based on SW distance.     [https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17417](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17417)

    本文提出一种基于数据库的分层验证方法，发现检测是纠正化学和材料推理中错误的关键瓶颈，门控修正可将错误率从22%降至4%。

    

    语言模型正进入化学和材料发现工作流程，在这些流程中，错误的分子式、空间群或形成能可能悄然传播到下游决策中。这些虚构内容隐藏在流畅的推理轨迹中，并集中在罕见的长尾实体上，而模型在这些实体上的置信度最不可靠。为每个提示检索参考数据可以捕捉这些错误，但会带来高昂的覆盖率和弃权成本。我们证明，确定性的、基于数据库的验证能够选择性地捕捉并修复这些错误，且约束瓶颈在于检测而非修复。我们的分层验证器提取每个可检查的声明，对照权威数据库和物理定律进行测试，仅在检查失败时检索参考值。在四个模型和超过五百个具有固定条件的提示中，门控修正将承诺分子式的错误率从22%降至4%，且速度提升3.2倍。

    arXiv:2607.17417v2 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Language models are moving into chemistry and materials discovery workflows, where a wrong molecular formula, space group, or formation energy can silently propagate into downstream decisions. These confabulations hide inside fluent reasoning traces and concentrate on rare, long-tail entities, where model confidence is least trustworthy. Retrieving reference data for every prompt would catch them, but at a heavy coverage and abstention cost. We show that deterministic, database-grounded verification catches and repairs these errors selectively, and that the binding constraint is detection rather than repair. Our tiered verifier extracts each checkable claim, tests it against authoritative databases and physical law, and retrieves a reference value only when a check fails. Across four models and over five hundred prompts with pinned conditions, gated correction cuts the error rate of committed formulas from 22% to 4% with 3.2 times fe

    arXiv:2607.08756v2 Announce Type: replace-cross  Abstract: We present MulTTiPop, a dataset of pop music segments and their associated multitrack MIDI recordings for the evaluation of automatic music transcription models. MulTTiPop contains 572 segments of popular music totaling 3.5 hours of audio, and contains songs from diverse genres and decades from the 1930s to 2000s. To collect this dataset, we perform metadata-based matching on song segments from the Lakh MIDI and TheoryTab datasets, manually identify an anchor beat between the audio and MIDI, then use beat tracking on the audio and warp the MIDI to match its tempo and timing. We evaluate state-of-the-art automatic music transcription models on MulTTiPop and find substantial room for improvement, with the best model achieving 29% Onset F1. More details and sound examples of MulTTiPop are available at https://gclef-cmu.org/multtipop.

    本研究表明，小型通用视觉-语言模型在零样本条件下即可达到与专用FRB检测器相当的准确率，且通过简单修改提示即可灵活适配多类任务，无需重新训练。

On a balanced binary benchmark of 2000 simulated L-band spectra, Gemma 4 E2B reaches an accuracy of 94.05\%, statistically indistinguishable from the specialized detector SwinYNet (92.85\%), with a far lower false-positive rate on structured RFI (4.8\% vs. 24.6\%) and none on pure noise, though SwinYNet ranks perfectly (ROC-AUC 1.0000 vs. 0.9520). Rewriting the prompt alone reconfigures the same models for three-class FRB/RFI/noise classification, reaching up to 86.0\% accuracy without a single false FRB while classifying each 2 s spectrum in 1.0--1.5 s, faster than the observation itself.     本文提出了一个名为VER的概念框架，用于系统性地监测学习表示中的持久性残余结构，以区分解释不充分性与常规噪声或不确定性。

    

    学习表示是现代机器学习中的核心，通常通过预测性能、鲁棒性、不确定性估计和泛化能力来评估。然而，一个学习表示可能在操作上成功，却未能组织传统评估指标未完全捕获的持久性残余结构。本文引入了VER（表征警觉评估器），这是一个用于监测表征充分性的概念框架。VER不提出新的学习算法、损失函数或模型架构，而是定义了一个诊断过程，用于识别持久性残余结构，并评估它们是否可能表明解释不充分性，而非不确定性、噪声、数据限制、局部模型误差或分布偏移。该框架包括五个操作：表示识别、解释域界定、残余结构评估（此处截断）。

However, a learned representation may remain operationally successful while failing to organize persistent residual structures not fully captured by conventional evaluation metrics. This article introduces VER (Vigilant Evaluator of Representations), a conceptual framework for monitoring representational adequacy. VER does not propose a new learning algorithm, loss function, or model architecture. Instead, it defines a diagnostic process for identifying persistent residual structures and assessing whether they may indicate explanatory insufficiency rather than uncertainty, noise, data limitation, local model error, or distribution shift.     arXiv:2606.08926v2 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Knowledge graph completion (KGC) models are commonly evaluated using rank-based metrics such as MRR and Hits@K, despite different users often requiring different evaluation perspectives. In this demo, we present PROBE-Web, an interactive system for probing diverse evaluation landscapes for KGC models. PROBE-Web enables users to flexibly evaluate KGC models by adjusting two critical perspectives: (P1) predictive sharpness and (P2) popularity-bias robustness. Through a user-friendly GUI, users easily evaluate multiple KGC models and analyze their strengths and weaknesses. PROBE-Web provides four key functionalities: (1) conventional evaluation toolkit, (2) flexible perspective-aware evaluation, (3) explainable case studies, and (4) evaluation landscape exploration. We believe that PROBE-Web can help users better understand KGC models aligning with their objectives.
    
[^158]: 表征涌现的引导理论：解释不足作为表征学习与世界模型的驱动力

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07303](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07303)

    该论文提出“表征涌现引导理论”，认为解释不足是驱动表征层级转换的关键信号，并描述了从稳定观察到表征涌现的递归五阶段过程。

    

    表征学习是现代机器学习的核心，但大多数研究在选定表征框架后专注于优化表征。较少关注何时需要新的表征层次。我们引入了表征涌现的引导理论（TBER），这是一个概念框架，其中持续的解释不足作为表征转换的信号。一个表征可能在保持描述性有用的同时，变得无法使相关观察、关系、转换或组织属性变得可理解。TBER描述了一个递归的五阶段过程：稳定观察、异常检测、解释不足识别、表征涌现和临时稳定。修订后的框架将涌现与验证区分开来：候选表征可能需要问题重新表征、判别性分析或进一步细化。

    arXiv:2606.07303v3 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Representation learning is central to modern machine learning, yet most research focuses on optimizing representations after a representational framework has been selected. Less attention is given to when a new representational level becomes necessary. We introduce the Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence (TBER), a conceptual framework in which persistent explanatory insufficiency acts as a signal for representational transition. A representation may remain descriptively useful while becoming unable to make relevant observations, relations, transformations, or organizational properties intelligible. TBER describes a recursive five-stage process: stabilized observation, anomaly detection, recognition of explanatory insufficiency, representational emergence, and provisional stabilization. The revised framework distinguishes emergence from validation: candidate representations may require problem re-representation, discriminat

    本文提出了“概念分配区”概念，表明Transformer中概念形成是跨深度的连续过程，并通过三个指标和自动边界检测实现了对概念形成位置和过程的精确追踪。

    

    概念形成在Transformer语言模型中是一个深度扩展的过程，而非单层事件：一个概念在一个或多个残差流的连续区域内变得可分离——该区域称为其概念分配区（CAZ）。CAZ不是概念本身，而是模型组织其几何结构以使概念可分离的深度段——概念可能共享CAZ，并通常跨越多个深度段；配套的GEM论文表明，分离方向在CAZ内部持续旋转，直到越过其边界后才稳定。我们通过三个逐层指标——分离度、概念连贯性和概念速度——来形式化CAZ，并采用自动边界检测，该检测不对CAZ成员资格施加显著性阈值（每个段都是CAZ；“强”与“温和”是分数，而非二元划分）。跨35个模型、8个架构家族和7个概念的实证验证表明，分离曲线S(l)经常是多模态的。

This survey provides a systems perspective on reproducibility failures across three modalities now dominant in financial AI: tabular models (post-hoc explanation variance), graph networks (stochastic sampling and temporal asynchrony), and LLM-based agentic workflows (batch-dependent divergence and trajectory drift).     arXiv:2605.22950v2 公告类型：替换-交叉 摘要：当归一化常数未知或评估成本过高时，分数匹配是最大似然估计的替代方法。然而，对于实际应用中常见的具有良好分离模式的多峰分布，普通分数匹配相对于最大似然估计已被证明效率较低。我们在此场景下比较了一种新颖的基于扩散的去噪分数匹配估计器（DDSME）与普通分数匹配估计器（SME）。特别地，我们为两种估计器证明了统计保证，表明当模式间分离度增加时，普通SME的误差界会恶化，而通过适当的超参数调整，DDSME可以避免这种情况。这为基于扩散的分数匹配优于普通版本提供了新颖的理论解释。我们通过数值实验支持了我们的理论发现。

    arXiv:2605.22950v2 Announce Type: replace-cross  Abstract: Score matching is an alternative to maximum likelihood estimation when the normalizing constant is unknown or too costly to evaluate. However, vanilla score matching has shown to be inefficient relative to maximum likelihood estimation for multimodal distributions with well-separated modes, which are commonly encountered in practical applications. We compare a novel diffusion-based denoising score matching estimator (DDSME) to the vanilla score matching estimator (SME) in this scenario. In particular, we prove statistical guarantees for both estimators, showing that the error bound for the vanilla SME worsens when the separation between the modes increases, which can be avoided in case of the DDSME with suitable hyperparameter tuning. This provides a novel theoretical explanation for the superior behavior of diffusion-based score matching over the vanilla version. We support our theoretical findings by numerical experiments.

    基于像素的深度强化学习代理通常在严重降采样的视觉观察上训练，这一惯例继承自早期基准测试，而非基于原理性设计。在本工作中，我们表明观察分辨率是策略学习中一个关键但被忽视的变量：只要网络架构能有效处理，更高分辨率的输入可以显著提升性能和泛化能力。我们发现广泛使用的Impala编码器将空间特征展平为向量，随着分辨率增加导致参数数量二次增长，且无法利用额外的视觉细节。我们测试了Impala架构的不同修改，并得出结论，特别是引入全局平均池化层（如Impoola架构）能在不同分辨率和网络宽度下带来一致的改进，同时将参数数量与分辨率解耦。

    arXiv:2605.10546v2 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Pixel-based deep reinforcement learning agents are typically trained on heavily downsampled visual observations, a convention inherited from early benchmarks rather than grounded in principled design. In this work, we show that observation resolution is a critical yet overlooked variable for policy learning: higher-resolution inputs can substantially improve both performance and generalization, provided the network architecture can process them effectively. We find that the widely used Impala encoder, which flattens spatial features into a vector, suffers from quadratic parameter growth as resolution increases and fails to leverage the additional visual detail. We test different modifications to the Impala architecture and conclude that, in particular, introducing a global average pooling layer, as in the Impoola architecture, yields consistent improvements across resolutions and network widths while decoupling parameter count from r

    arXiv:2605.05488v2 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: We propose an architecture that augments the Flux Neural Operator (Flux NO), which combines the classical finite volume method (FVM) with neural operators, with ViT-based context injection. Our model is formulated as a hypernetwork: it extracts solution dynamics over a finite temporal window, encodes them with a recurrent Vision Transformer, and generates the parameters of a context-conditioned neural operator. This enables the model to infer and solve conservation laws without explicit access to the governing equation or PDE coefficients. Experimentally, we show that the proposed method preserves the robustness, generalization ability, and long-time prediction advantages of Flux NO over standard neural operators, while delivering reliable numerical solutions across a broad range of conservative systems, including previously unseen fluxes. Our code is available at https://github.com/xx257xx/CONTEXT_FLUX_NO.

    类比推理是人类智能的标志性特征，使我们能够通过将知识从一个情境迁移到另一个情境来解决新问题。然而，开发能够进行稳健的类似人类的类比推理的人工智能系统已被证明是困难的。在这项工作中，我们使用元学习组合性（MLC）方法在类比推理任务（字母串类比）上训练Transformer，并评估其泛化能力。我们发现，当引导模型关注最具信息量的问题的元素时（通过在训练数据中加入复制任务来实现），字母串类比变得可学习。此外，当使用更多样化的数据集训练时，模型对新字母表的泛化能力得到改善。对于最佳训练运行，我们的3层编码器-解码器模型在字母串类比数据集上的表现与前沿模型相当。MLC方法还使模型能够在一定程度上进行泛化。

[^172]: 从反弹到补救：通过表征工程理解并缓解奖励黑客行为

    本研究通过环境操纵设置系统分析了LLM强化学习中的奖励黑客行为，发现三阶段反弹模式，并利用表征工程提取概念方向来追踪和缓解该现象。

    

    大型语言模型的强化学习容易受到奖励黑客行为的影响，即模型利用捷径来最大化奖励，而不解决预期任务。我们在编码任务中，通过环境操纵设置系统地研究这一现象，在该设置中，模型可以重写评估器代码，从而在不解决任务的情况下轻松通过测试，以此作为受控实验平台。在两个研究的模型中，我们识别出一个可复现的三阶段反弹模式：模型首先尝试重写评估器但失败，因为它们的重写嵌入的测试用例是其自身解决方案无法通过的。随后，它们暂时退回到合法求解。当合法奖励仍然稀缺时，它们以不同质的策略反弹到成功的黑客行为。利用表征工程，我们从领域通用的对比对中提取捷径、欺骗和评估意识的概念方向，发现捷径方向追踪了黑客行为。

We systematically study this phenomenon in coding tasks using an environment-manipulation setting, where models can rewrite evaluator code to trivially pass tests without solving the task, as a controlled testbed. Across both studied models, we identify a reproducible three-phase rebound pattern: models first attempt to rewrite the evaluator but fail, as their rewrites embed test cases their own solutions cannot pass. They then temporarily retreat to legitimate solving. When legitimate reward remains scarce, they rebound into successful hacking with qualitatively different strategies. Using representation engineering, we extract concept directions for shortcut, deception, and evaluation awareness from domain-general contrastive pairs and find that the shortcut direction tracks hac

    本文提出L4V方法，通过可微无线世界模型和离散伴随递归，将AAV轨迹学习中的不连续目标转化为可导代理，实现高效且精确的梯度传播。

L4V replaces a discontinuous completion-time objective with a cumulative-backlog surrogate, unrolls the mission dynamics, and propagates pathwise sensitivities to a neural policy through the discrete adjoint recursion. The resulting derivative is exact conditional on a fixed exogenous-noise realization; stochastic expected-objective optimization still requires sampling. We show that the structured adjoint grows at most polynomially with the horizon and establish a stationary-point rate for fixed-step full-gradient descent under standard s

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17853](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17853)

    本文提出神经先验估计器（NPE），从网络潜在表示中学习类别频率信号，无需显式类别计数，实现了与标准对数几率调整相当的性能，并提升了少数类别的分类效果。

    

    对数几率调整利用经验类别先验来纠正类别不平衡。我们研究是否可以从网络表示中学习到类似的类别频率信号，而无需显式地向校正规则提供类别计数。我们引入了神经先验估计器（NPE），它将一个或多个轻量级先验估计模块（PEMs）附加到潜在表示上。每个PEM在真实坐标上使用单向逻辑目标进行训练。由此产生的频率相关输出被组合成一个NPE估计，并用作学习到的对数几率校正，形成NPE-LA。在一个简化的标量模型中，PEM目标的最优解在类别数量上是单调的，并且渐近地增长为$\log N_c$，直到一个较慢的$\log \log N_c$项。在长尾CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，NPE-LA与标准对数几率调整具有竞争力，并且相比交叉熵（CE）改善了少数类别的性能。

    arXiv:2602.17853v2 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Logit adjustment corrects class imbalance using the empirical class prior. We study whether a comparable class-frequency signal can instead be learned from the network representation, without explicitly supplying class counts to the correction rule. We introduce the Neural Prior Estimator (NPE), which attaches one or more lightweight Prior Estimation Modules (PEMs) to the latent representation. Each PEM is trained with a one-way logistic objective on the ground-truth coordinate. The resulting frequency-dependent outputs are combined into an NPE estimate and used as a learned logit correction, forming NPE-LA. In a simplified scalar model, the optimum of the PEM objective is monotone in the class count and grows asymptotically as $\log N_c$, up to a slower $\log \log N_c$ term. Experiments on long-tailed CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that NPE-LA is competitive with standard logit adjustment and improves minority-class performance over CE
    
[^175]: 基于松弛参数优化损失函数引导扩散模型的逆向材料设计

    Guided Diffusion by Optimized Loss Functions on Relaxed Parameters for Inverse Material Design

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15648)

    本文提出一种基于扩散模型的新型逆向材料设计方法，通过将离散设计空间松弛为连续网格表示并利用隐式微分计算梯度，结合优化损失函数引导生成，有效解决了多模态逆向设计中的梯度不可用难题。

    

    逆向设计问题在工程和材料科学中很常见。正向方向，即从设计参数计算输出量，通常需要运行数值模拟（如有限元方法）作为中间步骤，这本身就是一个优化问题。在许多场景中，多个设计参数可能导致相同或相似的输出值。对于这种情况，多模态概率方法有利于获得多样化的解决方案。逆向设计的一个主要困难源于设计空间的结构，因为离散参数或进一步约束不允许直接使用基于梯度的优化。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种基于扩散模型的新型逆向设计方法。我们的方法将原始设计空间松弛为连续网格表示，其中梯度可以通过正向模拟中的隐式微分计算。一个扩散模型被训练以生成满足目标输出条件的松弛参数，并通过优化损失函数来引导生成过程，从而在保持多样性的同时实现精确的逆向设计。

The forward direction, i.e., computing output quantities from design parameters, typically requires running a numerical simulation, such as a FEM, as an intermediate step, which is an optimization problem by itself. In many scenarios, several design parameters can lead to the same or similar output values. For such cases, multi-modal probabilistic approaches are advantageous to obtain diverse solutions. A major difficulty in inverse design stems from the structure of the design space, since discrete parameters or further constraints disallow the direct use of gradient-based optimization. To tackle this problem, we propose a novel inverse design method based on diffusion models. Our approach relaxes the original design space into a continuous grid representation, where gradients can be computed by implicit differentiation in the forward simulation. A diffusion mo
    
[^176]: 一种统一物理信息神经网络，采用三阶段损失优化建模耦合电弹波传播

    A Unified Physics-Informed Neural Network for Modeling Coupled Electro- and Elastodynamic Wave Propagation Using Three-Stage Loss Optimization

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13811)

    本研究提出了一种统一物理信息神经网络，通过三阶段损失优化成功求解一维耦合电弹波传播问题，实现了位移和电势的低误差预测，验证了PINN作为无网格求解器的有效性。

    

    物理信息神经网络提供了一种科学机器学习中的新颖方法，通过损失函数中的软约束将偏微分方程形式的物理定律直接集成到神经网络中。本研究探讨了应用PINN求解一维耦合电弹动力学系统，该系统以应力-电荷形式建模线性压电性，由弹性和电动力学方程控制。我们的模拟采用前馈架构，将时空坐标映射到机械位移和电势。我们的PINN模型在位移和电势上分别实现了2.34%和4.87%的全局相对L2误差。结果验证了PINN作为耦合时变偏微分方程组有效无网格求解器的能力，尽管在耦合特征值系统中仍存在误差累积和刚性问题。

    arXiv:2602.13811v3 Announce Type: replace-cross  Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks present a novel approach in SciML that integrates physical laws in the form of partial differential equations directly into the NN through soft constraints in the loss function. This work studies the application of PINNs to solve a one dimensional coupled electro-elastodynamic system modeling linear piezoelectricity in stress-charge form, governed by elastodynamic and electrodynamic equations. Our simulation employs a feedforward architecture, mapping space-time coordinates to mechanical displacement and electric potential. Our PINN model achieved global relative L2 errors of 2.34 and 4.87 percent for displacement and electric potential respectively. The results validate PINNs as effective mesh free solvers for coupled time-dependent PDE systems, though challenges remain regarding error accumulation and stiffness in coupled eigenvalue systems.
    
[^177]: HBVLA：推动视觉-语言-动作模型的1位后训练量化

    本文提出HBVLA框架，通过策略感知增强Hessian和稀疏正交变换，在哈尔域进行分组1位量化，有效缩小二值化与全精度权重的分布差距，从而提升VLA模型在资源受限环境下的部署效率和长期动作准确性。

    

    视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型能够实现遵循指令的具身控制，但其巨大的计算和内存开销阻碍了在资源受限的机器人和边缘平台上的部署。虽然通过二值化将权重降低到1位精度可以大幅提升效率，但现有方法无法缩小二值化权重与全精度权重之间的分布差距，导致在长时间闭环执行过程中量化误差累积并严重恶化动作输出。为填补这一空白，我们提出了HBVLA，一个针对VLA定制的二值化框架。首先，我们使用策略感知增强Hessian来识别对动作生成真正关键的权重。然后，我们采用稀疏正交变换处理非显著权重，以诱导低熵中间状态。最后，我们在哈尔域中对显著和非显著权重进行分组1位量化。我们已评估了我们的方法。

Then, we employ a sparse orthogonal transform for non-salient weights to induce a low-entropy intermediate state.     本文提出一种基于量子核的矩阵乘法算法，其门复杂度为 \(O(N^2\log_2N)\)，优于经典最佳算法，并构建了统一的量子线性代数算子框架。

The investigation of their convergence properties is not only a major research topic during the early development of the RL field but also receives significant attention nowadays. This paper formally verifies their almost sure convergence in a unified framework based on the Robbins-Siegmund theorem. The framework developed in this work can potentially be extended to convergence rates and other modes of convergence. This work thus makes an important step towards fully formalizing convergent RL results. The code is available at https://github.com/ShangtongZhang/rl-theory-in-lean.
