    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07459](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07459)

    ProteinZero提出了一种基于在线强化学习的蛋白质生成框架，通过结合ESMFold结构指导和自推导ddG预测器的多目标奖励机制，并引入嵌入级多样性正则化，实现了高效、可扩展的自我改进蛋白质设计。

    

    arXiv:2506.07459v4 公告类型：替换 摘要：蛋白质生成模型在蛋白质设计中展现出显著潜力，但其成功率仍受限于对精选序列-结构数据集的依赖，以及监督目标与实际设计目标之间的错位。我们提出了ProteinZero，一个用于逆向折叠模型的在线强化学习框架，能够通过计算高效的反馈实现可扩展、自动化且持续的自我改进。ProteinZero采用了一个奖励流水线，结合了来自ESMFold的结构指导与一种新颖的自推导ddG预测器，提供稳定的多目标信号，同时避免了基于物理方法的高昂成本。为了确保在线强化学习的鲁棒性，我们进一步引入了一种新颖的嵌入级多样性正则化器，以缓解模式崩溃并促进功能上有意义的序列变异。在一个平衡多奖励优化与KL散度的通用强化学习公式中，我们展示了该方法的有效性。

    arXiv:2506.07459v4 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Protein generative models have shown remarkable promise in protein design, yet their success rates remain constrained by reliance on curated sequence-structure datasets and by misalignment between supervised objectives and real design goals. We present ProteinZero, an online reinforcement learning framework for inverse folding models that enables scalable, automated, and continuous self-improvement with computationally efficient feedback. ProteinZero employs a reward pipeline that combines structural guidance from ESMFold with a novel self-derived ddG predictor, providing stable multi-objective signals while avoiding the prohibitive cost of physics-based methods. To ensure robustness in online RL, we further introduce a novel embedding-level diversity regularizer that mitigates mode collapse and promotes functionally meaningful sequence variation. Within a general RL formulation balancing multi-reward optimization, KL-divergence from
    
[^191]: DeepConvContext：一种用于人类活动识别中时间序列分类的多尺度方法

    DeepConvContext: A Multi-Scale Approach to Timeseries Classification in Human Activity Recognition

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20894](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20894)

    本文提出DeepConvContext框架，通过多尺度建模窗口内和窗口间时间模式，显著提升了人类活动识别中的时间序列分类性能，平均F1分数提高5%，mAP提升最多18点。

    

    尽管公认在建模长期时间依赖性方面存在局限性，但人类活动识别（HAR）传统上依赖滑动窗口方法来分割标记数据集。像DeepConvLSTM这样的深度学习模型通常独立分类每个窗口，将可学习的时间上下文限制在窗口内信息，并产生碎片化、时间上不连贯的活动时间线。为解决这一限制，我们提出了DeepConvContext，一种用于HAR的多尺度时间序列分类框架。受基于视觉的时序动作定位社区的启发，DeepConvContext通过处理时间有序窗口序列，分别建模窗口内和窗口间的时间模式。在六个广泛使用的HAR基准测试中，DeepConvContext在F1分数上平均提高了5%，在mAP上比相关方法提高了最多18个百分点，同时实现了低延迟和高吞吐量。

    arXiv:2505.20894v3 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Despite recognized limitations in modeling long-range temporal dependencies, Human Activity Recognition (HAR) has traditionally relied on a sliding window approach to segment labeled datasets. Deep learning models like the DeepConvLSTM typically classify each window independently, restricting learnable temporal context to within-window information and producing fragmented, temporally incoherent activity timelines. To address this constraint, we propose DeepConvContext, a multi-scale time series classification framework for HAR. Drawing inspiration from the vision-based Temporal Action Localization community, DeepConvContext models both intra- and inter-window temporal patterns separately by processing sequences of time-ordered windows. Across six widely-used HAR benchmarks, DeepConvContext achieves an average 5% improvement in F1-score and up to 18-point improvement in mAP over related approaches, while achieving latency and throughp
    
[^192]: Table2Image：基于生成代理表示与可靠性诊断的轻量级表格学习

    Table2Image: Lightweight Tabular Learning with Generated Proxy Representations and Reliability Diagnostics

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06265](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06265)

    Table2Image通过生成代理表示和VIF初始化，在轻量级模型下实现竞争性预测性能，并提出了一个统一的不完美学习条件评估协议来诊断可靠性。

    

    深度表格模型理想情况下应平衡预测性能、参数效率和对不完美学习信号的鲁棒性——这些属性很少被联合考虑。我们提出了Table2Image，一种轻量级表格学习模型，其核心是一个学习生成通路，将表格输入映射为中间的结构化代理表示。我们还研究了一个变体，采用方差膨胀因子（VIF）信息初始化的方法，在训练开始时降低高度共线性特征的权重。在OpenML-CC18和TabZilla数据集上，Table2Image在保持竞争力的干净预测性能的同时，相对于多个大规模神经基线模型更加紧凑。我们进一步引入了一个统一的、严重程度受控的评估协议，涵盖三种不完美学习条件——无关输入、损坏监督和不稳定捷径关联——该协议结合了基于性能的鲁棒性度量。

    arXiv:2412.06265v3 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Deep tabular models should ideally balance predictive performance, parameter efficiency, and robustness to imperfect learning signals---properties that are rarely considered jointly. We present Table2Image, a lightweight tabular learning model built around a learned generation pathway that maps tabular inputs into intermediate, structured proxy representations. We additionally examine a variant with variance inflation factor (VIF)-informed initialization, which downweights highly collinear features at the start of training. Across datasets from OpenML-CC18 and TabZilla, Table2Image achieves competitive clean predictive performance while remaining compact relative to several large-scale neural baselines. [^193]: 虚拟传感实现难以到达位置与不可测量参数的实时监测

    Virtual Sensing to Enable Real-Time Monitoring of Inaccessible Locations & Unmeasurable Parameters

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00107)

    本文提出基于神经算子的虚拟传感框架MIMONet，从稀疏边界测量实时重建不可达内部场，在三个工程案例中误差低于5%且亚毫秒完成。

    

    对安全关键内部状态的实时监测是能源、环境和工业系统中的开放问题，在这些系统中直接仪表化不可行。基于控制方程、离散状态向量或固定传感器位置的方法无法在任意内部坐标处实时提供网格无关的场级重建。本文介绍了基于神经算子的虚拟传感框架，用于从稀疏边界测量中恢复不可达内部场，并通过MIMONet（一种多输入多输出神经算子）实现，该算子融合异构输入并通过共享潜在表示解码耦合场。在三个复杂度递增的工程级评估中——受限再循环流动、压水堆子通道和紧凑型电力系统换热器——MIMONet实现了相对重建误差低于5%，且亚毫秒级运行。

    arXiv:2412.00107v3 Announce Type: replace-cross  Abstract: Real-time monitoring of safety-critical interior states is an open problem across energy, environmental and industrial systems where direct instrumentation is infeasible. Approaches based on governing equations, discrete state vectors or fixed sensor locations cannot deliver mesh-independent, field-level reconstruction at arbitrary interior coordinates in real time. Here we introduce neural operator-based virtual sensing, a framework for recovering inaccessible interior fields from sparse boundary measurements, instantiated with MIMONet, a multi-input, multi-output neural operator that fuses heterogeneous inputs and decodes coupled fields through a shared latent representation. Across three engineering-grade evaluations of escalating complexity - confined recirculating flow, pressurized water reactor subchannels and compact power-system heat exchangers - MIMONet attains relative reconstruction error below 5% with sub-millisecon
    
[^194]: ReAugment：基于模型库引导的强化学习用于少样本时间序列增强与预测

    ReAugment: Model Zoo-Guided RL for Few-Shot Time Series Augmentation and Forecasting

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06282](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06282)

    本文提出ReAugment方法，利用强化学习自适应识别易过拟合样本并定向增强，以提升少样本时间序列预测性能。

    

    时间序列预测，特别是在少样本学习场景中，因高质量训练数据的稀缺而面临挑战。为解决此问题，我们开展了一项关于使用强化学习（RL）进行时间序列数据增强的初步研究。我们的方法ReAugment解决了三个关键问题：训练集的哪些部分应被增强、增强应如何进行，以及RL在此过程中带来哪些优势。具体而言，我们的方法维护一个预测模型库，并通过测量各模型间的预测多样性，识别出具有较高过拟合概率的样本，并将其作为增强的锚点。利用RL，我们的方法自适应地将易过拟合的样本转换为新数据，这不仅增强了训练集的多样性，还引导增强数据定向到预测模型易过拟合的区域。我们进行了验证...

    arXiv:2409.06282v5 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Time series forecasting, particularly in few-shot learning scenarios, is challenging due to the limited availability of high-quality training data. To address this, we present a pilot study on using reinforcement learning (RL) for time series data augmentation. Our method, ReAugment, tackles three critical questions: which parts of the training set should be augmented, how the augmentation should be performed, and what advantages RL brings to the process. Specifically, our approach maintains a forecasting model zoo, and by measuring prediction diversity across the models, we identify samples with higher probabilities for overfitting and use them as the anchor points for augmentation. Leveraging RL, our method adaptively transforms the overfit-prone samples into new data that not only enhances training set diversity but also directs the augmented data to target regions where the forecasting models are prone to overfitting. [^195]: 基于专家混合模型融合的高效帕累托集逼近方法

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09770)

    本文提出一种基于专家混合模型融合的帕累托集逼近方法，显著提高了大规模深度神经网络多目标优化的效率与可扩展性。

    

    arXiv:2406.09770v2 公告类型：替换-交叉。摘要：对于大型深度神经网络，求解多目标优化问题是一项具有挑战性的任务，这源于损失景观的复杂性以及训练和评估模型的高昂计算成本。高效逼近大型模型的帕累托前沿，能够支持多任务学习和权衡分析等多种应用中的多目标优化。现有的帕累托集学习算法，包括（1）进化算法、超网络和超体积最大化方法，计算成本高，且难以扩展到大型模型；（2）标量化算法，即为每个目标方向训练独立模型，这在学习整个帕累托集时效率低下，且无法有效捕捉目标间的权衡。受近期模型合并成功的启发，我们提出了一种基于专家混合（MoE）的实用且可扩展的帕累托集学习方法。

    arXiv:2406.09770v2 Announce Type: replace-cross  Abstract: Solving multi-objective optimization problems for large deep neural networks is a challenging task due to the complexity of the loss landscape and the expensive computational cost of training and evaluating models. Efficient Pareto front approximation of large models enables multi-objective optimization for various tasks such as multi-task learning and trade-off analysis. Existing algorithms for learning Pareto set, including (1) evolutionary, hypernetworks, and hypervolume-maximization methods, are computationally expensive and have restricted scalability to large models; (2) Scalarization algorithms, where a separate model is trained for each objective ray, which is inefficient for learning the entire Pareto set and fails to capture the objective trade-offs effectively.     提出一种新型双自动编码器模型(TAE)，通过将潜在表示转换为可分离表示来解决网络攻击检测中混合表示的问题

    

    表征学习在网络攻击检测等许多问题的成功中起着关键作用。大多数网络攻击检测的表征学习方法基于自动编码器（AE）模型的潜在向量。为了解决AEs表示中混合的问题，我们提出了一种称为双自动编码器（TAE）的新型模型。TAE将潜在表示确定地转换为更易区分的表示，即\textit{可分离表示}，并在输出端重建可分离表示。

    arXiv:2403.15509v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Representation Learning (RL) plays a pivotal role in the success of many problems including cyberattack detection. Most of the RL methods for cyberattack detection are based on the latent vector of Auto-Encoder (AE) models. An AE transforms raw data into a new latent representation that better exposes the underlying characteristics of the input data. Thus, it is very useful for identifying cyberattacks. However, due to the heterogeneity and sophistication of cyberattacks, the representation of AEs is often entangled/mixed resulting in the difficulty for downstream attack detection models. To tackle this problem, we propose a novel mod called Twin Auto-Encoder (TAE). TAE deterministically transforms the latent representation into a more distinguishable representation namely the \textit{separable representation} and the reconstructsuct the separable representation at the output. [^197]: 基于学习的方法从仅加速度计的惯性传感中估计速度

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.07468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.07468)

    CarSpeedNet模型利用智能手机加速度数据的时间窗口，无需额外传感器即可估计车辆速度，在4秒窗口下实现RMSE 1.8米/秒，并系统分析了窗口长度对估计误差的影响。

    

    arXiv:2401.07468v4 公告类型：替换交叉 摘要：提出的模型CarSpeedNet，从三轴智能手机加速度的时间窗口中估计标量车辆速度，在推理时无需陀螺仪、轮式里程计、车辆总线或定位输入。报告实验包含13.2小时的道路驾驶。除网络比较外，有限上下文分析将窗口长度视为传感问题的一部分。对于嵌套历史，最小贝叶斯均方误差随上下文不增加；互补的线索覆盖关系将同一窗口与呈现给网络的速度相关振动量及观测范围联系起来。在1秒输入下，CarSpeedNet与五种时间网络替代方案进行比较；随后在六个窗口长度上测量时间上下文的影响。在0.5小时保留集上，4秒窗口产生均方根误差（RMSE）为1.8米/秒，平均绝对误差（MAE）为0.72米/秒，而对比为2.9和1.3米/秒。

    arXiv:2401.07468v4 Announce Type: replace-cross  Abstract: The proposed model, CarSpeedNet, estimates scalar vehicle speed from a window of three-axis smartphone acceleration, without gyroscope, wheel-odometry, vehicle-bus, or positioning input at inference. At a 1-s input, CarSpeedNet is compared with five temporal-network alternatives; the effect of temporal context is then measured over six window lengths. [^198]: 基于工具变量的强化学习中的渐近理论及其潜在内生性

    Asymptotic Theory for IV-Based Reinforcement Learning with Potential Endogeneity

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2103.04021](https://arxiv.org/abs/2103.04021)

    本文提出基于工具变量的强化学习算法，以纠正数据生成与分析动态交互导致的“强化偏差”，并在随机逼近框架下建立其渐近理论。

    

    在标准数据分析框架中，数据是（一次性）收集的，然后进行数据分析。然而，随着数字技术的进步，决策者不断分析过去的数据，并通过他们的决策生成新数据。我们将此建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并表明数据生成与数据分析之间的动态交互会导致一种新型偏差——强化偏差，这种偏差加剧了标准数据分析中的内生性问题。我们提出了一类基于工具变量（IV）的强化学习（RL）算法来纠正这种偏差，并通过将其纳入随机逼近（SA）框架来建立其理论性质。我们的分析适应了迭代依赖的马尔可夫结构，因此可用于研究具有策略改进的RL算法。我们还提供了关于最优策略推断的公式。

    arXiv:2103.04021v4 Announce Type: replace-cross  Abstract: In the standard data analysis framework, data is collected (once and for all), and then data analysis is carried out. However, with the advancement of digital technology, decision-makers constantly analyze past data and generate new data through their decisions. We model this as a Markov decision process and show that the dynamic interaction between data generation and data analysis leads to a new type of bias -- reinforcement bias -- that exacerbates the endogeneity problem in standard data analysis. We propose a class of instrument variable (IV)-based reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms to correct for the bias and establish their theoretical properties by incorporating them into a stochastic approximation (SA) framework. Our analysis accommodates iterate-dependent Markovian structures and, therefore, can be used to study RL algorithms with policy improvement. We also provide formulas for inference on optimal policies of th
    
[^199]: 多模态图交互在多图卷积网络城市时空预测中的应用

    Multi-Modal Graph Interaction for Multi-Graph Convolution Network in Urban Spatiotemporal Forecasting

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/1905.11395](https://arxiv.org/abs/1905.11395)

    本文提出了一种多模态交互机制，通过分组GCN和特征交互技术，在多图卷积网络中有效整合多种空间关系，以提升城市时空预测的准确性并减少泛化误差。

    

    基于图卷积网络的方法最近被用于城市计算中区域级预测问题中的区域间关系建模。每种关系代表一种空间依赖性，如区域间距离或功能相似性。为了将多种关系整合到空间特征提取中，我们将该问题定义为多图卷积网络上的多模态机器学习问题。利用多模态机器学习的优势，我们提出为该问题开发模态交互机制，通过强化多模态协调表示的学习来减少泛化误差。在这项工作中，我们提出了两种交互技术，分别用于处理较低层和较高层的特征。在较低层中，我们提出了分组GCN，以结合来自不同模态的图连接性，实现更完整的空间特征提取。

    arXiv:1905.11395v2 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: Graph convolution network based approaches have been recently used to model region-wise relationships in region-level prediction problems in urban computing. Each relationship represents a kind of spatial dependency, like region-wise distance or functional similarity. To incorporate multiple relationships into spatial feature extraction, we define the problem as a multi-modal machine learning problem on multi-graph convolution networks. Leveraging the advantage of multi-modal machine learning, we propose to develop modality interaction mechanisms for this problem, in order to reduce generalization error by reinforcing the learning of multimodal coordinated representations. In this work, we propose two interaction techniques for handling features in lower layers and higher layers respectively.     本文证明了在随机最短路径问题中，乐观策略迭代算法在策略评估步骤采用蒙特卡洛或TD(λ)方法时，在终止状态几乎必然可达条件下的收敛性。

    

    本文证明了随机最短路径问题中乐观策略迭代算法的一个特例的收敛性结果。我们考虑了在终止状态几乎必然最终达到的条件下，策略评估步骤中蒙特卡洛方法和$TD(\lambda)$方法的收敛性。

    arXiv:1808.08763v3 Announce Type: replace  Abstract: In this paper, we prove some convergence results of a special case of optimistic policy iteration algorithm for stochastic shortest path problem. We consider both Monte Carlo and $TD(\lambda)$ methods for the policy evaluation step under the condition that the termination state will eventually be reached almost surely.
